# Карта на клиента: редизайн (the staff client card, redesigned)

> **Phase 4b (2026-10) — read `CLAUDE.md` Laws 1–9 for the current shape.** `libs/domain/*` and `libs/application/*` were deleted in Phase 4b (2026-10); links and paths into them below are dead history.

> Revision 3 (2026-09-29) supersedes parts of this file: see [client-card-revision-3.md](client-card-revision-3.md).

Status: design spec, **revision 2**, 2026-09-28. The lead designer's answer to the owner's five critiques of the client-card prototype, written against the lead's binding decisions D1–D8 and revised after three reviews (house rules and DS reuse; taste, HIG and Mobbin; feasibility and build plan). Every blocker and major of those reviews is resolved; the item-by-item record is the appendix **«Critique resolution»** at the end. Where this revision departs from the letter of a D-decision, §0 says so, says why, and gives the one-line fallback.

**What this supersedes.** This file replaces, in `docs/design-research/client-details-design.md` (below: **the old spec**):

Mobbin links are cited inline where a pattern is taken.

Each is the design default this spec builds; each has a one-line fallback that changes no other section.

The old spec's binding ruling kept the last visit above the fold (`client-details-design.md:2186-2215`, `:2643`); `prototype-critique.md` flagged the cut note below the fold; Mobbin puts the newest item big and first (Uber, Fresha: `mobbin-history-and-sections.md` §2.4). The reward stays above it (taste pre-flight #6)                                                                                                                                                                                                | Swap the two module blocks back; every module is its own component                                             |

| DV5  | D2.5 copy «още 60 т. до …»                                                                                                                      | The points sentence names the best reward the balance already covers («Стига за Безплатно подстригване»); «Още N т. до …» only when nothing is affordable. **Held rule (wave D r5–r6):** a reward the client already holds on this visit — a grant E offers (`grants`) or one already on the draft bill (`grantsOnBill`) — is never the one the points are «enough for»; the next-best affordable one is, else «Още N т. до …». The sentence therefore reads the same at rest, after «Използвай» and after «Премахни» (Георги: «Стига за Безплатно оформяне на брада» beside his free-haircut grant) | For Георги (≈308 т., rewards 120/250/400) the D2.5 sentence names the one reward he cannot have; [TheFork](https://mobbin.com/screens/bf26ab37-0135-4930-a123-9d764169de0f) and [Ulta](https://mobbin.com/screens/5e1db78b-5bb0-4336-841e-7ed0d962030f) label what the points buy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Always the «Още …» sentence                                                                                    |

A tile caption is a label, not a fact line, so R59 (numeric «12.08» in sentences and fact lines) still holds everywhere else                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Month grain for the staff list (DV2's fallback), which needs no month word                                     |
| DV10 | DV6: «Пак същото» on the repeat tile                                                                                                            | The repeat quick action reads **«Повтори»** («Repeat», `clientCard.actions.repeat`); `appointments.visit.bookAgain` «Пак същото» stays the client app's sentence-length button                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | In the four-tile row at the 355 pt column «Пак същото» wraps to two lines while the other three labels hold one; tile labels are one word, as in Contacts and Telegram (lead's visual review, 2026-09-28)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Three tiles when the verb is absent (unchanged rule)                                                           |

A shelf where some tiles lose their caption reads as a fault, and the caption is how a shelf is scanned (a digest repeats its items in their sections: Apple Fitness awards, the App Store). Within the first screen the rail no longer repeats anything: the season track stays off «Скоро» while its card words it, and «Ново» marks «Последни» only | Drop the caption (`captioned: false`) on a shelf tile that «Скоро» already shows                               |

Bulgarian `Intl {month:'short'}` is numeric («09»), which reads as a code under a mark, and `{month:'long'}` («септември») does not fit a period's column at 390. Dates elsewhere stay `Intl`: the record's month is `Intl`'s own month form («септември 2026 г.», the head's «от март 2025 г.»), and a ladder node's earlier year is `Intl`'s «2025 г.» under its «15.04», never a table word or a year sliced out of the day                                                                                                                                                                                            | Numeric captions («09»)                                                                                        |

A notch tied to its reward needs a glyph mark `ui-mark-scale` does not draw. The glance's points tile (F1) keeps the balance on a notched bar                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | A muted card holding the bar and the rows, once `UiMark` carries a glyph                                       |
| DV14 | (wave D r4 review, for sign-off) §5.1.11 / DD: reached step nodes are the tint with `--sys-color-on-accent` numerals; the next node a tint ring | **A level passed is the solid tint with the registry check** (`checklist.done`, 16, on-accent: a mark), and prints no numeral; only the **current** node prints one, in `--sys-color-accent-label` on the tint's wash inside its tint ring; the next node and those ahead are ringed in the secondary ink, never the tint (the next one's numeral in the primary ink). The same for the tier track (LH), the season track (AF, CE) and the ladder (DD)                                                                                                                                               | White type on the tint is 3.04:1 (axe, r3): under AA at the caption size. r3 answered it by moving every numbered reached node to the wash, which drew earned I–IV as the faintest discs on the track (1.17:1 on the card) while the unearned next node wore a crisp accent ring, so a finished ladder read as unreached, and the tier track (blank nodes, solid) drew «reached» the other way. A check on a filled step is the stepper grammar (the level stays in the hidden level list, the words under the node and the medal's rim), and a graphic needs 3:1 (SC 1.4.11), which the white check on the tint meets                                                                                 | Dark numerals on the solid tint (black on #f26b22 is 6.9:1) — a second on-accent ink the palette does not have |

## 1. The owner's critique, answered

| #   | Owner critique (intent)                                                                               | What was wrong (measured)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Design answer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Where                   |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| 1   | The head is hand-rolled: no collapsing title, no scroll-edge fade like the other pushed pages         | The prototype drew its own `.bar` and `.head`; its compact title never appeared. In the real app the fade flips on at ≈60 px while the compact title lands at ≈120 px, so the big name ghosts behind the translucent chips under an empty bar (`app-capture/light-53-search-collapse-strip.png`, frames 040–080) | The name is the one `h2 uiSheetLargeTitle id="staff-visit-page-title"` inside `ui-sheet-headline`. The DS collapse is fixed for every sheet page: one timeline measured from the bar's bottom edge drives a **cross-fade**: the large title and the headline media fade out over the first half of the exit, the compact title fades in over the second half, the top fade rises across the whole range. The two names are never visible together | §3.1, §5.1.1, §5.1.2    |
| 2   | The name is far bigger than the avatar                                                                | 36/800 name (~10,300 px² of ink) beside a 52 px disc (~2,100 px²): 5× the mass                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | The name is `title2` 24/700; the avatar (`large`, 52) moves to a **trailing** media slot. The text column has a 52 pt floor and centres inside it, so a one-line name + state line (48.6 pt) balances the disc exactly; a longer name grows downward while the disc stays on its first line ([Revolut Business](https://mobbin.com/screens/473ee23d-dda1-49b8-9770-8386494eba4d)). No measuring in the feature                                    | §3.1, §5.1.1            |
| 3   | Gamification is one cramped card; domains have no home; achievements cannot scale to Battlefield size | Four domains in one 358×95 button, 7 px punch dots, a fixed 6-medal grid                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Each domain gets its own place: a **Лоялност** module (points tile, stamp card tile, rhythm tile) that pushes a full «Лоялност» page; a compact **Постижения** strip that pushes a collection built to scale: diversity-ruled rails, category shelves of tiered families (I–V) drawn with their own art, a season card with a step track, events, records, a year archive one push away, scope pages and a medal detail with a step ladder        | §3.1 F–G, §3.2–§3.5, §4 |
| 4   | Everything is dropped into list groups and rows                                                       | 11 blocks at one 16 px gap; 4 of 6 list groups hold one row                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Every domain is a designed module under a sheet-sized heading: capsules, quick-action tiles, a coupon ticket, a featured visit card with plain rows, a bento of metric tiles, a medal strip, a photo rail. The glance page has one list group (the exits); the loyalty page two (catalogue, ledger), never adjacent; achievements, scope, detail and history pages none                                                                           | §3                      |
| 5   | «Всички посещения» re-invents a list the client app already has                                       | The history page was generic 3-line rows; the client app's `ClientAppointments` already has the row, the month grouping and the filter                                                                                                                                                                           | Row, sections, filter predicate and filter menu move to a shared lib `@creativo/features/shared/visits` with `perspective`, `grain` and `listStyle` inputs; «Моите часове» mounts it with no behaviour lost; staff add status by typography, the price paid, the barber, «Този час», marks and a stats band. The date stamp is promoted to the DS as `ui-date-tile`                                                                               | §3.6, §5.3              |

## 2. Page map and the navigation stack (D6)

### 2.1 Page kinds

`EditorPageKind` (`libs/features/staff/dashboard/src/lib/visit-editor/staff-visit-editor.ts:745`) gains five kinds (NEW): `'clientLoyalty' | 'clientAchievements' | 'achievementCategory' | 'achievementDetail' | 'clientHistory'`. `photo` and `discount` are reused. `EditorPage` (`:787`) gains two fields (NEW):

```ts
interface EditorPage {
  readonly kind: EditorPageKind;
  readonly subjectId: string | null;
  readonly originTestId: string | null;
  /** The page this one was pushed from; null for a page pushed from the ladder. (NEW) */
  readonly returnTo: EditorPage | null;
  /** The sheet scroller's scrollTop when this page was pushed, restored on pop. (NEW) */
  readonly returnScrollTop: number;
}
```

`subjectId` per kind: `client`, `clientLoyalty`, `clientAchievements`, `clientHistory` carry the `DraftClient.id`; `achievementCategory` carries a **scope key**: `category:{BadgeCategoryId}`, `season:{SeasonId}`, `archive`, or `day:{YYYY-MM-DD}` (levels earned that day, §3.3 AE); `achievementDetail` carries the `BadgeSeriesId`; `photo` carries the `photoId`; `discount` keeps today's meaning. The client whose card is open is the nearest `client` entry down the `returnTo` chain (`clientOfStack()`, NEW private helper).

| kind                                    | reached from (origin test id)                                                                                                         | depth                                              | bar title (compact)                                                                            | ‹ goes to                                       | dock ✓ goes to                         | focus on arrive                                                           | focus on return (after ‹ from its child)                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `client`                                | ladder row `staff-visit-client-row-{id}`                                                                                              | 1                                                  | the person's display name                                                                      | ladder                                          | ladder                                 | the page container `.staff-visit__page`                                   | the origin inside the card (the tile, medal, row or thumb that pushed)                                                     |

| `clientAchievements`                    | `client-achievements-all`                                                                                                             | 2                                                  | «Постижения»                                                                                   | `client`                                        | ladder                                 | container                                                                 | the medal, card or row that pushed                                                                                         |
| `achievementCategory`                   | `achievements-shelf-{categoryId}-all`, the season card, an event card, the archive row, a day group; from the archive, a season medal | 3 (a season opened from the archive: 4)            | the scope's title («Майсторство», «Есен 2026», «Минали сезони и събития», «Спечелени на 4.09») | its `returnTo`                                  | ladder                                 | container                                                                 | the tile that pushed the detail                                                                                            |

A related medal **replaces** this page (same depth, same `returnTo`)                                           |

| `scan` (reused)                         | the discount page's scan button                                                                                                       | **same depth** as `discount` (a replace, as today) | existing                                                                                       | `discount` (a replace back)                     | existing                               | existing                                                                  | the code field, as today                                                                                                   |
| `clientSearch` → `clientNew` (existing) | unchanged: `clientNew` **replaces** `clientSearch`                                                                                    | 1, as today                                        | existing                                                                                       | `clientSearch` (a replace back, the query kept) | existing                               | existing                                                                  | the query field, as today                                                                                                  |

### 2.2 The stack rules

Three primitives, one invariant (the chain survives every move):

- **`push(kind, subjectId, originTestId)`** stops refusing when a page is up (today `if (this.page()) return;`, `:2757`). It records `returnTo = page()` and `returnScrollTop` = the sheet scroller's `scrollTop` (the nearest `.modal-sheet__scroll` ancestor of the host), then does exactly what it does today: clears `query` and `searchFocused` (`:2758-2760`), sets the page, `scrollPageToTop()`, focuses the container.
- **`replace(kind, subjectId, originTestId)`** (NEW) swaps the top page at the **same depth**: the new page inherits the replaced page's `returnTo` and `returnScrollTop`; it does **not** clear `query`/`searchFocused` and does not reset scratch. It is what `openNewClient` (`:3726-3733`), the scanner's opener, `pop()`'s `clientNew → clientSearch` branch, `returnToDiscountPage()` (`:4338`) and therefore `scanned()` (`:4652`) now call instead of a bare `page.set(...)`, so a `client → clientLoyalty → discount → scan` chain comes back to `clientLoyalty` intact. A related medal on a detail page and a push that would reach depth 5 also replace. `staff-visit-editor.spec.ts:4983` (`depth()` is 1 with the scanner up) stays true unchanged.
- **`pop()`** keeps its two special branches (now through `replace`, so the typed query survives, as `:2789-2797` promises) and gains one generic branch: the page being left runs its scratch reset (`servicePicks`, `resetDiscountEntry`, `librarySelection`, `:2809-2811`); when `returnTo` is set, `page.set(returnTo)`, then inside `afterNextRender` (the parent page's body renders synchronously from cached store signals) write `returnScrollTop` back to the scroller and focus the popped page's `originTestId` with `preventScroll: true`. Restoring before the render would clamp to the child page's shorter height. With no `returnTo`, today's behaviour (`page.set(null)`, focus the origin).
- **Depth cap 4.** `depth = computed(() => chain length)` (public, `:1738`; the host keeps stamping `data-depth`).
- **Dock ✓ on every client-stack page = back to the visit.** `confirmPage()` (`:1802`) on `client`, `clientLoyalty`, `clientAchievements`, `achievementCategory`, `achievementDetail`, `clientHistory` and a read-only `photo` calls a NEW `popToLadder()`: it unwinds the whole chain, restores the root's `scrollTop` (in `afterNextRender`) and focuses the ladder row that pushed `client`. ‹ walks one step; ✓ leaves the client. Two controls, two intents.
- **✕, Escape and drag** close from any depth behind the discard guard (built rule, `staff-dashboard.ts` `dismissVisitSheet()`), unchanged.
- **Party hops** (`visitTrail`, the dashboard's) are unaffected: the ‹ shows when `depth() > 0 || visitTrail().length > 0` (`staff-dashboard.html:1157`), unchanged.

### 2.3 Focus and scroll, stated once

- Arrive: the page container (`tabindex=-1`), never a control (house rule). The loyalty page opened from a tile focuses that tile's section heading (`h3 tabindex="-1"`) after scrolling it under the bar, clear of the chrome's fade (`scroll-margin-block-start: var(--modal-sheet-clear-top)` — the scroller's published chrome height plus the fade's 3rem overhang; flush at the bar's edge the heading arrived ghosted inside the fade, wave D r2).
- Return: scroll restored first (after the render), then the origin element focused with `preventScroll`. Origins carry `scroll-margin-block: var(--modal-sheet-origin-margin)` (the clear top above, a 52 dock and its regular air below — `var(--modal-sheet-clear-top) calc(var(--control-size-large) + var(--sys-space-regular) * 2)`, published once by `ui-modal-sheet`), so a keyboard user who moves on never lands under chrome or its fade.
- The sheet's one polite region (`announce`) is unchanged: pages open none of their own.

---

## 3. Pages

### 3.0 Frame and conventions (all pages)

- **Frame.** 390 × 844 pt phone, visit sheet full height. Bar 61 pt (52 control + padding), content starts 16 under it (y 111). Page inset `--ui-sheet-inset` = `--modal-sheet-safe` = 1.1rem (17.6 pt) at ≤760 px, so the **column is 354.8 pt** (called 355 below). The dock starts at ≈776.
- **Gaps.** Modules are separated by the sheet's own page gap, `--sys-space-comfortable` (24, the `.staff-visit__page` gap, reused: no page-local stack). Inside a module, heading → body is `compact` (8). The head → capsule row is `regular` (16).
- **The type ladder.** Every page the client stack pushes titles with the sheet's `largeTitle` 36/800, as every pushed page of the visit sheet does; the two heads that carry a 52 pt face, the glance's name and the medal detail's family title, are `title2` 24/700, sized with the face (D1's pair; DV7 resolved to its fallback). Both through `uiFont` on the projected `h2` (the house practice: all six existing sheet pages set their `h2`'s `uiFont`). Under it, on the glance page: module figures `title3` 20/600 (`ui-metric uiControlSize="regular"`) > module headings `headline` 18/600 (`ui-section-header uiHeaderProminence="increased"`) > row titles `callout` 600 > facts `footnote` 12 > captions `caption` 11. The only `ui-metric large` (`title` 28/700) in the stack is the «Лоялност» page's balance: a page about points leads with the points. Tabular figures everywhere (re-asserted after every `font:` shorthand).
- **One title role per page** (ruling, WP7 review r4, 2026-09-28; supersedes r1's «one title role per chain»). A page keeps its title role wherever it is pushed from: the discount page and the scanner that replaces it are `largeTitle` from the ladder's discount row and from a ready ticket (glance E, «Лоялност» LD) alike, bound as the static `uiFont="largeTitle"` (the editor's per-origin `stackTitleFont()` is gone). A new page the client stack can push is `largeTitle` unless its head carries headline media, which makes it `title2` (the pair rule above). (The photo page's `h2` is visually hidden; the bar names the photo from rest.)
- **The glance budget** (taste pre-flight row 21, §9): at the development seed, 390 pt wide, every flag on, the glance page's content runs **≤ 1,400 pt from the head's top** (≈2 screens) and shows **≤ 40 visible text runs**, not counting the figures drawn inside graphics (date-tile captions and days, medal rim numerals). §3.1.1 carries the count; WP8 recounts from the screenshots.
- **Wireframe legend.** `╭╮╰╯` a `ui-list-group`'s outer corners; `┊` a segment seam; `▢` a muted `ui-card` / tile (`--sys-color-surface-secondary`, `--control-radius-prominent`); `( )` a circle; `[ ]` a `span[uiBadge]`; `›` pushes (`nav.disclosure`); `↗` leaves the sheet (`nav.leave`); `⏷` the filter funnel (`action.filter`); `▒` `ui-skeleton`; `‹role›` role-gated. In the tables, `increased` / `standard` is shorthand for `uiHeaderProminence`, `ui-card muted` for `uiTone="muted"`.
- **Ink words** are `uiForegroundStyle` values (primary, secondary, accent = `--sys-color-accent-label`, destructive). Meter and medal **fills** use `--sys-color-accent`, the family every meter already uses (§5.1.15). No hex in feature CSS.
- **Absence.** Data-less or role-less = absent, never disabled (house). A module whose slice fails keeps its heading and shows a compact `ui-notice` with «Опитай пак» under it (one retry grammar, §3.1.4).
- **One fact, once.** No value is printed twice on one screen (house ruling, memory `money-rows-single-row-push-page`); each region below names the one place a fact lives.

Above the fold: who; how to reach them; the reward to use now; **what we did last time, with its photo and the client's words; when they come next**. Loyalty starts one flick down. Order is the barber's three-second questions (old spec §4.5, DV1), the reward ahead of history (taste pre-flight #6).

**Budget check (§3.0).** Content 111 → 1,496 = **1,385 pt** (≤ 1,400). The prototype measured ≈1,070 pt with fewer modules; draft 1 of this spec ≈1,650 pt and ≈60 runs.

**Module failure** (any of H, F, G, I): the module's heading stays, its body becomes a compact `ui-notice uiTone="neutral"` with `[uiNoticeActions]` «Опитай пак» (`visits.retry`): «Лоялността не се зареди.» (`client-retry-loyalty`, likewise `-achievements`, `-visits`, `-photos`). The domain keeps its place; no lone retry row.

#### 3.1.3 Behaviours

- **Tiles push with an anchor.** F1 → `clientLoyalty` at «Каталог»; F2 → at «Карти с печати»; F3 → at «Ритъм»; F0's «Всички» → the page top (Setel's per-strip push, [Setel](https://mobbin.com/screens/25f69654-dc9a-4d11-b5b5-ee57def75b64)).
- **Medals push the detail directly** (depth 2); «34 ›» pushes the collection.
- **«Пак същото»** as old §5A.1.3 (legs from the last visit's `{serviceId, variantId, minutes}`, pop to the ladder, the polite line «Взехме услугите от 4 септември.»). The body emits `repeatLast(legs)`; the editor applies it through the existing draft machinery.
- **Reward ticket (changed routing).** The discount page lists only the **booker's** grants (`staff-dashboard.ts:3922` `probeGrants(row.ownerUserId)` → `staff-dashboard.html:1189` `[uiGrants]`), and the server refuses a grant that is not the appointment owner's (`apps/functions/src/use-cases/staff-edit-appointment.use-case.ts:826-869`). So E (and «Лоялност» LD) render **only when the card's subject is the appointment owner**, from the editor's own `uiGrants` less those already on this bill, and push today's unfiltered discount page, which lists exactly those grants. **The ticket does what its verb says** (WP7 review r5): «Използвай» puts that grant on the bill through the discount page's own card path (`applyOffer`; a grant the bill cannot take beside what stands on it is refused, and the page shows it blocked) and then pushes the page with it applied — «Приложени» names it, its card is stamped with «Премахни» where «Използвай» stood; the dock's ✓ goes to the visit, ‹ to the card, where **the same ticket stands stamped where it stood** — the discount page's own applied card, the check on its glyph, «Премахни» where «Използвай» stood — and takes the focus (WP7 review r6: it used to vanish, the layout shifting under the restored scroll and the focus falling to the page container). The card's «Премахни» runs the discount page's own way off (`removeDiscount` on the grant's line) and the ticket, offered again in place, keeps the focus. Nothing «Използвай» uses leaves the card; the page container takes the focus only when an origin is really gone. It used to push the page only, which then asked «Използвай» again over «Приложени · Няма». A companion's held rewards are not shown in this build (owner question Q9).
- **Waitlist line (changed rule).** The server writes no `booked` state today (`libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/waitlist-request.ts:215` has the transition, nothing calls it), so a request can read "open" after the client booked. The line renders only when the request is watching and not lapsed **and** no upcoming visit of this person has a `bookedAt` at or after the request's `createdAtIso`. The rest (the `{{when}}` grammar, dayparts, absent while paused) is old §5A.1.3.
- **Photos** push the reused `photo` page read-only (§3.7).
- **The destructive row** runs `removeClient(id)` (one act for a party and for a party of one: `withoutClient` decides), pops to the ladder, focuses «Добави клиент» or the next person (old §5A.1.3). The template comment that «a destructive row would need copy `staff.visit` does not carry» (`staff-visit-editor.html:69-74`) is answered by reusing `staff.visit.removeClient`.
- **Nothing on this page writes** outside the draft.

#### 3.1.5 ≥761 px (centred dialog)

A 576 px dialog with a 24 inset: **528 px column**. Title column 460. Quick-action tiles ≈126 wide, same row. Loyalty: the wide tile keeps its row, the half tiles ≈260 each. The medal strip keeps 3 medals (one object) and the cue gets one line. Photo rail `uiContainerRelativeCount` 11 / span 2 (5.5 visible). Nothing else reflows.

---

### 3.2 «Лоялност» (D3; replaces the old «Награди» page)

The page answers four questions in order: what can they spend, what can they use now, what the points buy, how they progress. Each section has its own visual grammar (one domain, one visual: [Airalo](https://mobbin.com/screens/1ce2dd9b-73b7-4ce5-b663-1f414f248a59), [Qantas](https://mobbin.com/screens/40df7347-ca51-41f4-9831-239b6388fb5d)). The tier is named once, by its capsule in the summary; the tier track below names the whole scale.

#### 3.2.1 Wireframe: Георги, barber viewer

```
 y    ┌──────────────────────────────── 390 pt ─────────────────────────────────┐
  42  │ (‹)                           Лоялност                            (✕)   │ bar
 111  │ Лоялност                                                                │ LA largeTitle 36/800 (the sheet's page title)
      │ Георги Петров                                                           │ LB subheadline · secondary
 175  │ ▢──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────▢   │ LC ONE summary tile · ui-card muted
      │ │ ★ 308 т.                                               [◆ Злато] │   │    ui-metric large · tier capsule (flag loyalty.tiers)
      │ │ за размяна                                                       │   │
```

#### 3.2.3 Behaviours, states, ≥761 px

- **Anchors.** `anchor ∈ 'catalog' | 'stamps' | 'rhythm' | null` scrolls that section's heading under the bar and focuses it (`h3 tabindex="-1"`), as §2.3.
- **Order and flags.** A section whose data is absent is absent (no heading). `loyalty.points` → LC figure, LE, LI; LD follows the grants alone (live data, no flag; owner only); `loyalty.tiers` → the LC capsule and LH; `loyalty.achievements` → LF; `loyalty.streaks` → LG.
- **Paused (A15).** A compact `ui-notice uiTone="neutral" uiIcon="loyalty.freeze"` «Замразено, докато записването е спряно» (`rewards-frozen`) under the head; LD absent; LE rows keep costs but no «още …»; the rhythm renders `uiFrozen`; values are never zeroed.
- **Loading.** LC one 88 pt block; each section one block shaped like its card; the ledger five 60 pt rows.
- **Error.** Per section: the heading + the compact retry notice (`rewards-retry-{section}`); the whole account failing → `ui-notice` «Лоялността не се зареди.» [Опитай пак] (`rewards-retry`).
- **Offline.** C″ under the head; tickets absent (using one is a write).
- **≥761 px.** 528 column; the stamp card keeps 5 per row (slots never stretch past 1.5rem); rhythm shows 8 periods; the catalogue's mark labels spread. Nothing else reflows.

---

### 3.3 «Постижения» (D4)

The collection at Battlefield scale. The principle every large catalogue on Mobbin shares: the profile holds one summary object; the collection sits one push away and is a stack of category shelves; tiers collapse a family into one medal; seasons and events are dated containers, archived by year ([Reddit shelves](https://mobbin.com/screens/bf14becb-1dc6-4f40-90f1-db3dc8bfaf85), [Duolingo tiers](https://mobbin.com/screens/27423508-e523-49a9-a2bd-2c8eabe9e661), [Apple Fitness «Go For It»](https://mobbin.com/screens/385c1711-6119-4b91-8ec2-50851648825a), [Life Reset season](https://mobbin.com/screens/ba9bcc35-dc05-4f69-b3d6-f82e3149aa59), [Duolingo year archive](https://mobbin.com/screens/c305d492-71c0-433b-99e7-71309ec14278)). What keeps it from turning into noise at scale: rails that never show two faces of the same visit (§4.2 diversity rule), families drawn with their own art (a barber's face, a service's cover), and counts in the unit the shelf draws.

**The numeral rule** (written into `ui-medal`, §5.1.13): the rim numeral is always the **highest earned** level and is absent when none is earned; a target appears only in words, in the caption («24/25 до III»); titles never carry a numeral. Levels are roman numerals I–V, never Бронз/Сребро/Злато (those are client tiers).

#### 3.3.3 Behaviours and rulings

- **Counting (supersedes R31's 12-badge cap for recognition badges, D4).** The unit of the summary is a **level**: each level is its own once-rule under its own award key (`badgeNeedsOnce` holds per level). Shelves and scope pages count **families**, the unit they draw: the printed «N от M» is families EARNED (a level — the filled discs) of the total (wave D r6: a bare started count read as earned beside medals); started is printed only worded («· 3 от 9 започнати», the scope subline, CB). No page shows a total of levels with a denominator (DV3). R31's 12-published guidance applies only to badge rules with non-empty `awards`.
- **«с това посещение».** When the visit on screen is open and its legs would complete a level (`target − value ≤ 1` on a metric the visit moves: visits, visits with one of today's services, barber or location, an early-bird visit that starts by 10:00), the caption reads «с това посещение». Presenter-only; nothing is written.
- **Ethics kept (binding).** No hidden badges; not started = the real emblem or art as a grey silhouette in a dashed ring, never a padlock; no leaderboards, no rarity %; no countdown copy (a date range, «до 30.11» at most); staff never see a celebration (a quiet «Ново» only); «серия» banned, the word is «в ритъм».
- **Emblems and art.** Glyphs come from the closed icon registry (`loyalty.badge.<emblem>`, §8.2). Art is data, not a remote icon remap (the registry ruling holds): a barber family draws `Barber.avatar` (`libs/domain/catalog/src/lib/barber.ts:50`), a service family `Service.cover` (`service.ts:112`), a shop family the location's `cover`; without art, a family about one thing draws a monogram (the avatar-fallback grammar): a service family its title's letter («Фейд» → «Ф»), a barber or shop family the letter of the name the catalogue gives the barber or the shop («Иван» → «И», `Location.shortName` «Център» → «Ц»; their titles are phrases, «С Иван», which would give every barber an «С»); a family about no one thing, or about a subject the catalogue no longer knows, draws its emblem. Owner art (`BadgeSeries.art`, a Storage path) wins over all of these. **One face per family** (review, 2026-09-28; wave D r6 made it catalogue-wide and about the FACE, not the emblem): no two families of one shelf or one season/event share a face, and a season's families never repeat its track's; the rhythm shelf is `event_repeat` · `cycle` · `u_turn_left`, «Преди отпуска» `luggage` (emblems `yearRound`, `comeback`, `vacation`, §8.2). **Seasons and events wear faces of their own** (wave D r6, Critique 3): a season — its track, its card, its archive row, its slot on the glance — is drawn with its season of the year (`summer` `sunny`, `autumn` `eco`; `spring`/`winter` join with the first season the catalogue opens in them, since no emblem is dead vocabulary), so a year's archive is four faces and «Лято 2026» III is never the running «Есен 2026»; an event keeps `event` `celebration`. A season's or an event's family is a limited edition: it never draws a perennial family's face — not the emblem it counts like (the «Ранобудник» sunrise, the «Съботен» sofa, the «Препоръки» group), not its service's cover or letter (the cover is «Оформяне на брада»'s) — but an emblem of its own: «Всеки летен месец» `sailing`, «Лятна събота» `icecream`, «Всеки есенен месец» `umbrella`, «Есенно утро» `foggy`, «Приятел наесен» `handshake`, «Есенна брада» `self_care`, «Брада на седмицата» `health_and_beauty`, «Пълна грижа на седмицата» `spa`; and no two of them, in any season or event of one year, share one (`season` `award_star` and `seasonMonthly` `date_range` retired). **A picture two families share** (a shop that reuses a photo: the seed's «Пълна грижа» wears «Класическа подстрижка»'s cover, «Баща и син» «Модерна визия»'s) belongs to the first family in the catalogue's order; the other draws its letter — on every page alike. Pinned by `achievements.presenter.spec.ts` «one face per family» and «… across seasons and shared pictures», and over the seeded catalogue by `tools/seed/loyalty.spec.mjs`.
- **Taps.** A medal pushes `achievementDetail`; a shelf's action, the season card, an event card, a day group and the archive row push `achievementCategory` (depth 3).

#### 3.3.4 States and ≥761 px

| state                               | delta                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| loading                             | AC a 70 pt block; each rail four 52 pt discs; the season card one 170 pt block; shelves two rails                           |
| nothing earned yet (account exists) | AE absent; AD leads with the first levels, footnote «Първото постижение идва с първото посещение.»                          |
| no season running                   | AF absent; AC's fact line omits it                                                                                          |
| no open event                       | AG absent (an announced event is a card with «от 1.11» and no progress)                                                     |
| catalogue failed                    | `ui-notice` «Постиженията не се заредиха.» [Опитай пак] (`achievements-retry`)                                              |
| progress failed, catalogue loaded   | the same notice: drawing every medal `notStarted` would be a fake zero                                                      |
| paused                              | no delta: progress freezes on the server (A15)                                                                              |
| ≥761 px                             | rails `uiContainerRelativeCount` 11 / span 2 (5.5 visible); scope grids 5 columns; the season card's families row shows all |

### 3.4 Scope page (`achievementCategory`)

One body, four scopes: a **category** (sectioned by what the families are about), a **season**, the **archive**, a **day**. The mastery scope is sectioned by `ServiceCategory` («Коса», «Брада», «Грижа»), so forty services never become one flat wall of identical discs (the [stoic «all badges»](https://mobbin.com/screens/a0c81122-b698-4de6-86f3-5f8fb3133166) anti-pattern); within a family the same art with its numeral is fine ([Quicken](https://mobbin.com/screens/b957db39-b831-46c8-bd9e-4d0d95ceb14e)).

```

      │ (фейд)           ( К )            ( М )                                 │ CD ui-grid adaptive 6rem · button[uiMedal] large
      │  ⁽ᴵᴵ⁾                                                                    │    art: the service cover, else a monogram
      │ Фейд             Класическа       Модерна визия                          │
      │ 24/25 до III     0/3 до I         0/3 до I                               │
      │ Брада                                                        1 от 2     │
      │ (брада)          ( О )                                                  │
      │  ⁽ᴵ⁾                                                                     │
      │ Оформяне на брада Освежаване                                            │
      │ 6/10 до II       0/3 до I                                               │
      │ Общи                                                                    │    families with no service (Изследовател)
```

### 3.5 Medal detail (`achievementDetail`)

The Garmin / Reddit / Duolingo anatomy ([Garmin](https://mobbin.com/screens/4b83593e-4774-4406-925b-188979a822fe), [Duolingo locked](https://mobbin.com/screens/cf300909-93e0-42c1-9612-5ef9e96d0dea)) drawn with the house head: the medal rides the same trailing `[uiHeadlineMedia]` slot as the client's avatar (one head, one alignment axis), the state is the headline's one line, and the ladder is **one horizontal object**, not a list that repeats the requirement five times (the Forest failure, `mobbin-achievements-scale.md` §2.3; the fix: [Me+](https://mobbin.com/screens/cd9cb0ab-c1c3-447f-b528-7a9894e16d62), [Swarm 1x→2x→3x](https://mobbin.com/screens/edb44d83-1786-493c-b2f7-c00c1ec0f432), [Tripadvisor ruler](https://mobbin.com/screens/c96b10a3-7058-447e-a2b2-a5df034982fb)).

Why not `ui-detail-sheet` (`libs/ui/controls/src/lib/detail-sheet`, "tap a thing, read about it"): it is its own `ui-modal-sheet`, a second sheet over the visit sheet. D6 makes the detail a page pushed inside the visit sheet, so it takes that sheet's DS head.

Behaviours: upcoming rows are the first page's rows with start ≥ now and an open status (no second query). «Този час» marks the appointment on screen wherever its date puts it. The filter applies to the **subject's outcome** in memory over the loaded rows (`matchesVisitFilter`, §5.3.2); when it leaves fewer than 10 rows and a cursor remains, the list fetches up to three more pages on that tap. The list opens no visits (old R7; «Поправи изхода» lives with the visit).

States: loading = HC two 40×24 blocks, rows five 60 pt placeholders; filtered empty = `ui-empty-state` (bare, the client's form) with «Няма посещения за този филтър.» (`appointments.empty.filtered`) and «Покажи всички» (`history-filter-clear`); whole failure = `ui-notice` «Посещенията не се заредиха.» [Опитай пак] (`history-retry`); offline = C″ notice, HG absent; paused = no delta; long strings = titles truncate (full text in the row's name), the fact line never wraps. ≥761 px: HC shows the admin's spend as a 5th cell instead of the footnote.

### 3.7 Photo page reuse (`photo`)

The existing `photo` page (`staff-visit-editor.html:869-970`) is reused **read-only** for a past cut: `subjectId` = `photoId`, `returnTo` = the `client` page. `currentPhoto` (`staff-visit-editor.ts:4442`) looks up `vm().photos` first, then `store.photoById(photoId)` (a `ClientPhotoVm`, §5.2.1), which the **editor** maps to its own `VisitEditorPhoto` (`:477-490`): `barberName` from `ClientCardCatalog.barberName(barberId)`, `clientLabel` = `store.displayName()`, `origin: 'shot'`, `label: null` (the card lib cannot import the dashboard's type: that would be a cycle). The dock shows ✓ only (no «Изтрий снимката»: the photo belongs to its own visit). Bar title: the date «12.08» (R59), shown from rest; the chrome's fade is not (it rises with the scroll, §5.1.2), so the picture is never veiled at rest (wave D r5). The «Снимано» row says only what the bar does not, the time («10:15», the shop's clock); the visit's own photo page follows the same split (bar «4.09», row the time), so the date is never printed twice (wave D r4). Focus returns to the thumbnail.

| state                                 | «Лоялност»                                                                                                                       | «Постижения», scope, detail                                                                           | «Посещения»                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| guest / walk-in                       | unreachable (no loyalty, achievements or visits module for a guest)                                                              | unreachable                                                                                           | unreachable                                                                                              |
| new client, 0 visits (account exists) | welcome points in LC; LF «0 от 10»; LG «Първото посещение започва ритъма.»; LH on «Бронз», «още 6» at Сребро; LI the welcome row | unreachable from the glance (G absent); a deep push lands on the «nothing earned yet» state of §3.3.4 | HC «0» «посещения» + «Първо посещение»; «Предстоящи» with «Този час»; HH «Това е първото посещение.»     |
| no loyalty account                    | unreachable (F and G absent)                                                                                                     | unreachable                                                                                           | unaffected                                                                                               |
| long name                             | the subline truncates to one line; the page title is the page's name                                                             | same                                                                                                  | same                                                                                                     |
| party member                          | every read keys on the member's own uid; LD absent (§3.1.3)                                                                      | same                                                                                                  | rows show only the member's legs; the quote only on visits the member booked; «с още N» in the fact line |
| offline                               | C″ under the head; cached values; nothing that writes                                                                            | same                                                                                                  | same; HG absent                                                                                          |
| paused                                | §3.2.3 (frozen notice, no tickets)                                                                                               | no delta                                                                                              | no delta                                                                                                 |
| loading, empty, error                 | §3.2.3                                                                                                                           | §3.3.4, §3.4, §3.5                                                                                    | §3.6                                                                                                     |

---

## 4. The achievements and loyalty model (D4, D7)

> **Superseded (`CLAUDE.md` Laws 2 and 7):** ids are branded primitives (`Brand<string, 'CustomerId'>` with a `Result` smart constructor), never `Id<'…'>` classes; there are no classes, mappers or `*.errors.ts` outside Angular, and errors are flat `{ errorCode }` structs. The paragraph below records the 2026-09 style; `docs/architecture/domain-model.md` and the memory note it cites were retired (the doc deleted in Phase 4b).

The house domain style holds (memory "rich domain models"; the deleted `docs/architecture/domain-model.md`): ids are `Id<'…'>` classes with `create()` → `Result`; things with invariants are classes with private constructors, validating factories and `*.errors.ts`; wire shapes are `*Document` interfaces in the application layer, parsed by mappers that go through those factories. The **per-client projections** (account, family progress, season progress, ledger rows, records) stay flat readonly interfaces, deliberately, on the `ClientStats` precedent (`libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/client-stats.ts:73`): they are server-written projections the web app never mutates, and the behaviour over them is cross-aggregate (catalogue × progress), so it lives in pure domain services beside them, exactly as `statWindows(stats, now)` lives beside `ClientStats`.

### 4.1 Domain types (`libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/loyalty/`, NEW folder)

```ts
// ── ids.ts (additions to libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/ids.ts, the CouponId pattern) ──
export class BadgeCategoryId extends Id<'BadgeCategory'> {
  static create(raw: string): Result<BadgeCategoryId, EmptyIdError>;
}
export class BadgeSeriesId extends Id<'BadgeSeries'> {
  static create(raw: string): Result<BadgeSeriesId, EmptyIdError>;
}
export class SeasonId extends Id<'Season'> {
  static create(raw: string): Result<SeasonId, EmptyIdError>;
}

// ── badge-vocabulary.ts: closed, code-level unions (a new value is a release) ──
export type BadgeCategoryKind =
  'milestones' | 'mastery' | 'barbers' | 'rhythm' | 'special' | 'records';
export type BadgeEmblem =
  | 'visits'
  | 'streak'
  | 'referral'
  | 'selfService'
  | 'birthday'
  | 'welcome' // model.ts
  | 'service'
  | 'barber'
  | 'shop'
  | 'anniversary'
  | 'earlyBird'
  | 'weekend'
  | 'onTime'
  | 'waitlist'
  | 'profile'
  | 'party'
  | 'explorer' // NEW
  | 'yearRound'
  | 'comeback'
  | 'vacation'
  | 'event' // NEW (one face per family, §3.3.3)
  | 'summer'
  | 'autumn' // a season of the year (wave D r6)
  | 'sailing'
  | 'icecream'
  | 'umbrella'
  | 'fog'
  | 'handshake'
  | 'grooming'
  | 'beardOil'
  | 'spa'; // seasonal families' own (wave D r6)
export type Metric =
  | 'client.visits.completed'
  | 'client.bookings.selfService'
  | 'client.spend.major'
  | 'client.referrals.completed'
  | 'client.waitlist.booked'
  | 'client.points.lifetime'
  | 'shop.bookings.created' // model.ts
  | 'client.services.distinct'
  | 'client.barbers.distinct'
  | 'client.locations.distinct'
  | 'client.months.distinct'
  | 'client.visits.earlyBird'
  | 'client.visits.weekend'
  | 'client.visits.onTime'
  | 'client.visits.party'
  | 'client.tenure.days'; // NEW
export interface MetricRef {
  readonly metric: Metric;
  readonly window?: { readonly lastDays: number };
  readonly where?: {
    readonly serviceId?: ServiceId;
    readonly barberId?: BarberId;
    readonly locationId?: LocationId;
  }; // NEW
}
export type AwardSummary =
  // what a level paid, for «донесе …»: a summary of RuleBody.awards
  | { readonly kind: 'points'; readonly points: number }
  | { readonly kind: 'coupon'; readonly title: LocalizedText }
  | { readonly kind: 'freeze'; readonly count: number }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'perk';
      readonly perkId: string;
      readonly forDays: number | null;
    };
export type BadgeSubject =
  | { readonly kind: 'service'; readonly serviceId: ServiceId }
  | { readonly kind: 'barber'; readonly barberId: BarberId }
  | { readonly kind: 'location'; readonly locationId: LocationId };

// ── badge-category.ts ──
export class BadgeCategory {
  private constructor(
    readonly id: BadgeCategoryId,
    readonly kind: BadgeCategoryKind,
    readonly title: LocalizedText,
    readonly order: number,
  ) {}
  static create(
    props: BadgeCategoryProps,
  ): Result<BadgeCategory, BadgeCategoryError[]>; // 'orderNegative'
}

// ── badge-series.ts: ONE medal family, the unit the UI draws ──
export interface BadgeLevel {
  // a value inside the family, validated by it
  readonly level: number; // 1-based, drawn «I»…«V»
  readonly ruleId: string; // the once-rule that awards it (badgeNeedsOnce holds per level)
  readonly target: number | null; // on the family's metric; null = a one-off act
  readonly requirement: LocalizedText; // «25 посещения с фейд»
  readonly awards: readonly AwardSummary[];
}
export class BadgeSeries {
  private constructor(/* every field below */);
  static create(
    props: BadgeSeriesProps,
  ): Result<BadgeSeries, BadgeSeriesError[]>;
  // errors: 'noLevels' · 'levelGap' (1…n contiguous) · 'targetsNotRising' · 'homeless' (neither a
  // category nor a season) · 'oneOffWithSeveralLevels' · 'repeatableWithSeveralLevels'
  readonly id: BadgeSeriesId;
  readonly categoryId: BadgeCategoryId | null;
  readonly seasonId: SeasonId | null;
  readonly title: LocalizedText; // «Фейд»
  readonly requirement: LocalizedText; // the family's sentence, «Посещения с фейд» (§3.5 DC)
  readonly emblem: BadgeEmblem;
  readonly subject: BadgeSubject | null;
  readonly metric: MetricRef | null; // null for one-off acts
  readonly art: { readonly path: string } | null; // owner art in Storage; wins over subject art and emblem
  readonly levels: readonly BadgeLevel[];
  readonly repeatable: boolean; // perPeriod 'year' (birthday)
  readonly order: number;
  readonly retiredAt: Date | null;
  readonly version: number;
  standing(progress: BadgeProgress | null): SeriesStanding;
  isRetired(): boolean;
  isCalendarDriven(): boolean; // tenure: nothing a visit does moves it
  levelAt(level: number): BadgeLevel | null;
}

// ── season.ts: a named, dated window (Europe/Sofia, inclusive); an event is a short one without a track ──
export type SeasonPhase = 'announced' | 'open' | 'ended';
export class Season {
  private constructor(/* every field below */);
  static create(props: SeasonProps): Result<Season, SeasonError[]>;
  // errors: 'windowInverted' · 'eventWithTrack' · 'seasonWithoutTrack' · 'announcedLate' (R29 notice)
  readonly id: SeasonId;
  readonly kind: 'season' | 'event';
  readonly title: LocalizedText;
  readonly emblem: BadgeEmblem;
  readonly window: { readonly from: IsoDate; readonly until: IsoDate };
  readonly trackSeriesId: BadgeSeriesId | null;
  readonly seriesIds: readonly BadgeSeriesId[];
  readonly announcedAt: Date;
  readonly version: number;
  phaseOn(today: IsoDate): SeasonPhase;
}

// ── loyalty-catalog.ts: the published catalogue, validated as one read model ──
export class LoyaltyCatalog {
  private constructor(/* … */);
  static create(parts: {
    categories: readonly BadgeCategory[];
    series: readonly BadgeSeries[];
    seasons: readonly Season[];
    tiers: readonly TierDefinition[];
    rewards: readonly RewardCatalogItem[];
    streaks: readonly StreakDefinition[];
  }): Result<LoyaltyCatalog, LoyaltyCatalogError[]>;
  // errors: 'danglingCategory' · 'danglingSeason' · 'duplicateId' · 'tiersNotRising'
  category(id: string): BadgeCategory | null;
  series(id: string): BadgeSeries | null;
  seriesIn(categoryId: string): readonly BadgeSeries[];
  season(id: string): Season | null;
  seasonsIn(phase: SeasonPhase, today: IsoDate): readonly Season[];
  readonly categories: readonly BadgeCategory[];
  readonly tiers: readonly TierDefinition[];
  readonly rewards: readonly RewardCatalogItem[];
  readonly streaks: readonly StreakDefinition[];
}
// TierDefinition, RewardCatalogItem, StreakDefinition, TierState, StreakState: model.ts's shapes as readonly
// value interfaces, validated by LoyaltyCatalog.create (tiers rising, costs positive).

// ── projections.ts: per client, server-written (flat, the ClientStats precedent) ──
export interface LoyaltyAccount {
  // loyaltyAccounts/{uid}
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly balance: number;
  readonly lifetimeEarned: number;
  readonly lifetimeAdjusted: number;
  readonly lifetimeSpent: number;
  readonly tier: TierState;
  readonly streaks: Readonly<Record<string, StreakState>>;
  readonly punchCards: readonly {
    readonly ruleId: string;
    readonly filled: number;
    readonly size: number;
    readonly title: LocalizedText;
  }[];
  readonly records: readonly PersonalRecord[];
  readonly pointsExpiry: {
    readonly expiresOn: IsoDate;
    readonly noticeFrom: IsoDate;
  } | null;
  readonly seq: number;
  readonly updatedAt: Date;
  // CHANGED vs model.ts: no `badges` summary. The glance reads the progress documents (≤ one per family)
  // and derives latest/closest at read, so the strip and the page can never disagree (§4.2).
}
export interface BadgeProgress {
  // loyaltyAccounts/{uid}/badgeProgress/{seriesId}; absent = not started
  readonly seriesId: string;
  readonly value: number;
  readonly earned: readonly {
    readonly level: number;
    readonly earnedAt: Date;
    readonly via: 'rule' | 'manual';
    readonly count: number;
  }[];
}
export interface SeasonProgress {
  readonly seasonId: string;
  readonly trackValue: number;
  readonly trackLevel: number;
}
export type PersonalRecordKind =
  'longestRhythm' | 'mostVisitsInYear' | 'favouriteService' | 'firstVisit';
export interface PersonalRecord {
  readonly kind: PersonalRecordKind;
  readonly value: number;
  readonly on: IsoDate | null;
  readonly year: number | null;
  readonly serviceId: string | null;
}
export interface LedgerEntry {
  // a point-moving clientJournal row
  readonly id: string;
  readonly seq: number;
  readonly at: Date;
  readonly delta: number;
  readonly label: {
    readonly code: string;
    readonly params: Readonly<Record<string, string | number>>;
  };
  readonly reverses: string | null;
}
```

`LocalizedText`, `ServiceId`, `BarberId`, `LocationId` come from `@creativo/domain/catalog` (reuse, never copy: a badge family points at catalogue things; `domain → domain` is a legal edge and catalog imports nothing from engagement, so no cycle). The legacy `Achievement` class in `libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/achievement.ts` is **not touched**: the new names are `Badge*`.

### 4.2 Standing: pure domain services (`libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/loyalty/standing.ts`, NEW, each with a spec)

```ts
export type MedalState = 'notStarted' | 'inProgress' | 'earned';
export interface SeriesStanding {
  readonly state: MedalState; // earned = at least one level earned
  readonly highestLevel: number; // 0 = none (the rim numeral, §5.1.13)
  readonly next: BadgeLevel | null; // null = complete
  readonly value: number;
  readonly target: number | null;
}
export interface ClosestLevel {
  readonly seriesId: string;
  readonly level: number;
  readonly value: number;
  readonly target: number;
}
export interface EarnedDay {
  readonly day: IsoDate;
  readonly levels: readonly {
    readonly seriesId: string;
    readonly level: number;
    readonly earnedAt: Date;
  }[];
}

export function levelNumeral(level: number): string; // 1 → «I» … 10 → «X»
export function earnedLevelCount(
  progress: ReadonlyMap<string, BadgeProgress>,
): number;
export function familyCounts(
  catalog: LoyaltyCatalog,
  progress: ReadonlyMap<string, BadgeProgress>,
  scope: FamilyScope,
  today: IsoDate,
): {
  readonly started: number;
  readonly earned: number;
  readonly total: number;
};
export function closestLevels(
  catalog: LoyaltyCatalog,
  progress: ReadonlyMap<string, BadgeProgress>,
  today: IsoDate,
  limit: number,
): readonly ClosestLevel[];
export function recentLevelsByDay(
  progress: ReadonlyMap<string, BadgeProgress>,
  limit: number,
): readonly EarnedDay[];
export function isNewSince(
  earnedAt: Date,
  previousVisitAtMs: number | null,
): boolean;
export function bestAffordable(
  rewards: readonly RewardCatalogItem[],
  balance: number,
  today: IsoDate,
): RewardCatalogItem | null; // costliest affordable
export function nextAffordable(
  rewards: readonly RewardCatalogItem[],
  balance: number,
  today: IsoDate,
): RewardCatalogItem | null; // cheapest cost > balance
export function tierSteps(
  state: TierState,
  tiers: readonly TierDefinition[],
): {
  readonly steps: readonly {
    readonly tierId: string;
    readonly atLeast: number;
    readonly name: LocalizedText;
  }[];
  readonly value: number;
  readonly currentTierId: string;
  readonly nextTierId: string | null;
  readonly remaining: number | null;
};
export function redeemProblem(
  item: RewardCatalogItem,
  ctx: {
    balance: number;
    today: IsoDate;
    paused: boolean;
    recent: readonly RedemptionRecord[];
  },
): RedeemProblem | null; // moved verbatim from model.ts
export type FamilyScope =
  | { readonly kind: 'category'; readonly categoryId: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'season'; readonly seasonId: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'archive' }
  | { readonly kind: 'day'; readonly day: IsoDate };
```

**`closestLevels`, the diversity rule** (one visit moves four to six families at once; without this the rails become four clones of «24/25»):

1. Candidates: families that are not complete, not retired, not in an ended season, not calendar-driven (`isCalendarDriven()`: tenure), with a known next target; ranked by `value / target`, descending.
2. **At most one family per category** (a season's families count as that season).
3. Among families whose next level completes on the **same next worked visit** (equal `target − value` on a metric every worked visit moves), keep only the **most specific subject**: service › barber › location › none; ties by category `order`, then series `order`.
4. Fill to `limit` in rank order.

**`recentLevelsByDay`** groups earned levels by the Europe/Sofia calendar day of `earnedAt`, newest day first; a day with several levels is one rail item (§3.3 AE).

### 4.3 Where it lives: reuse vs a new lib (decided)

| Candidate                                                | Holds today                                                                                                                                          | Verdict                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `libs/domain/engagement` (`scope:shared`, `type:domain`) | `Points`, `Reward`, the legacy `Achievement`, `Milestone`, `RewardProgram` (date-windowed), `RewardProgress`, coupons, grants, vouchers, invitations | **Reuse.** It is the loyalty bounded context already. The read model goes in the NEW folder `src/lib/loyalty/` (§4.1–§4.2), ids in the existing `ids.ts`. Gains an import of `@creativo/domain/catalog` (its `tsconfig.lib.json` reference is added by the owner, §7)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `libs/domain/programs`                                   | `Course`, `Position`, `ShopEvent` (marketing programmes)                                                                                             | **Not reused**: a marketing `ShopEvent` is a public listing, not a loyalty window                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| a new `libs/domain/loyalty`                              | —                                                                                                                                                    | **Rejected**: it would split one context in two and re-mint ids `engagement` owns                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `libs/application/engagement`                            | coupon/grant/voucher/invitation/reward-progress ports; `gift-voucher-document.ts` (a wire precedent)                                                 | **Reuse** for the two loyalty ports, `loyalty-document.ts` and the mappers (§6). Features reach the domain only through it (eslint: `type:feature` may not import `type:domain`), so it re-exports the read-model types and functions **by name**, never `export *` (the `DiscountBreakdown` precedent in its `index.ts`). A NEW `src/testing.ts` entry (`@creativo/application/engagement/testing`) carries the fixtures and in-memory readers (the `@creativo/application/booking/testing` precedent) |
| `libs/application/booking`                               | appointment ports; `client-stats-document.ts`; `testing.ts`                                                                                          | **Reuse** for the history and photo ports; their in-memory readers join `testing.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

The legacy `users/{uid}/rewardProgress` read (`REWARD_PROGRESS_READER`, owner-only rule) stays the client's welcome programme and is not used by staff.

### 4.4 Wire documents and the shared fixtures (the contract WP3 and WP6 build against)

`libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/loyalty-document.ts` (NEW) declares one `*Document` interface per collection plus the collection-name constants: `BadgeCategoryDocument`, `BadgeSeriesDocument`, `SeasonDocument`, `TierDefinitionDocument`, `RewardCatalogItemDocument`, `StreakDefinitionDocument`, `LoyaltyAccountDocument`, `BadgeProgressDocument`, `SeasonProgressDocument`, `ClientJournalEntryDocument`. Wire rules, stated once:

- **Ids** are document ids and are **not** repeated in the body, except `userId` where a rule or a query reads it (`loyaltyAccounts`, `clientJournal`).
- **Instants** are ISO-8601 UTC strings (`"2026-09-04T07:12:00.000Z"`), never Firestore `Timestamp`s (model.ts's `IsoInstant`); **dates** are Europe/Sofia `"YYYY-MM-DD"`; **points** are integers; **`LocalizedText`** is a `{ "bg": string, "en": string }` map.
- Arrays keep their model names: `levels[]` (`{level, ruleId, target, requirement, awards[]}`), `earned[]` (`{level, earnedAt, via, count}`), `records[]`, `punchCards[]`, `seriesIds[]`.
- The journal's `effect` is model.ts's single `JournalEffect` object, so `effect.kind` is queryable (§6.1).

**Fixtures** (NEW, `libs/application/engagement/src/testing/fixtures/loyalty/`): `catalog.json` (every catalogue collection, keyed by collection then id), `account-georgi.json`, `account-martin.json` (account + `badgeProgress` + `seasonProgress` + `clientJournal` docs). They are parsed by WP3's mapper specs, by WP6's projector spec (the projector's output must deep-equal their **shape**, not their numbers) and by WP7's e2e seed helper. One file, three readers: the wire format cannot drift between packages.

### 4.5 Firestore layout

| Collection                                        | Document                     | Written by                                   | Read rule (NEW)                                                                                        | Size             |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------- |
| `badgeCategories/{categoryId}`                    | `BadgeCategoryDocument`      | server (`publishRule`, P3); the dev seed now | `allow read: if isSignedIn(); allow write: if false;`                                                  | 6                |
| `badgeSeries/{seriesId}`                          | `BadgeSeriesDocument`        | server / seed                                | same                                                                                                   | 42 in the seed   |
| `seasons/{seasonId}`                              | `SeasonDocument`             | server / seed                                | same                                                                                                   | 3 in the seed    |
| `tierDefinitions/{tierId}`                        | `TierDefinitionDocument`     | server / seed                                | same                                                                                                   | 4                |
| `rewardsCatalog/{rewardId}`                       | `RewardCatalogItemDocument`  | server / seed                                | same                                                                                                   | 3                |
| `streakDefinitions/{streakId}`                    | `StreakDefinitionDocument`   | server / seed                                | same                                                                                                   | 1                |
| `loyaltyAccounts/{uid}`                           | `LoyaltyAccountDocument`     | server / seed                                | `allow read: if worksTheBook(); allow write: if false;` (owner read joins with `loyalty.selfView`, P5) | 1 per client     |
| `loyaltyAccounts/{uid}/badgeProgress/{seriesId}`  | `BadgeProgressDocument`      | server / seed                                | nested match, same rule                                                                                | ≤ one per family |
| `loyaltyAccounts/{uid}/seasonProgress/{seasonId}` | `SeasonProgressDocument`     | server / seed                                | nested match, same rule                                                                                | ≤ one per season |
| `clientJournal/{uid}_{seq:06}`                    | `ClientJournalEntryDocument` | server / seed                                | `allow read: if worksTheBook() && resource.data.visibility == 'client'; allow write: if false;`        | append-only      |

Catalogue documents are presentation, not personal data, so any signed-in person may read them (clients will, in P5). Progress and the journal are staff-only now. Every write is the server's (Admin SDK).

### 4.6 Dev seed content (the engine's output, derived from the seeded history)

The seed stays honest: loyalty numbers are **derived** from the same appointments the seed writes, by a pure projector (`tools/seed/loyalty.mjs`, NEW, `projectLoyaltyFor(uid, appointments, catalog, today)`), so they can never disagree with `clientStats`. Only `dev-client-1` Георги and `dev-client-2` Мартин get accounts; everyone else has none (their modules are absent). The **catalogue** collections are part of `--catalog-only` (the e2e global setup runs that mode, `apps/web/e2e/support/global-setup.ts:39-47`; `tools/seed-dev-catalog.mjs:44-56` lists what it covers, and loyalty's catalogue joins that list); accounts, progress, journal, grants, photos and the waitlist request are written by the full run only.

**Categories** (`badgeCategories`)

| id               | kind       | bg                | en              | order | heading glyph                                                        |
| ---------------- | ---------- | ----------------- | --------------- | ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `cat-milestones` | milestones | Етапи             | Milestones      | 1     | `loyalty.category.milestones` (`signpost`)                           |
| `cat-mastery`    | mastery    | Майсторство       | Mastery         | 2     | `loyalty.category.mastery` (`design_services`)                       |
| `cat-barbers`    | barbers    | Бръснари и салони | Barbers & shops | 3     | `loyalty.category.barbers` (`groups`)                                |
| `cat-rhythm`     | rhythm     | Ритъм             | Rhythm          | 4     | `loyalty.category.rhythm` (`timeline`)                               |
| `cat-special`    | special    | Специални         | Special         | 5     | `loyalty.category.special` (`star_shine`)                            |
| `cat-records`    | records    | Рекорди           | Records         | 6     | `loyalty.category.records` (`trophy`): no families; the records rail |

**Families** (`badgeSeries`, 42): targets per level I…V; awards only where a level pays (all others are recognition-only and cost nothing). Art: barber families draw the barber's avatar, service families the service cover, shop families the location cover (§3.3.3), so the seed needs no owner art. A season's or an event's family draws its own emblem instead of its subject's art (wave D r6), and a season its season of the year.

| ----- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------- |
