Requirement copy is generated from the metric: «{{target}} посещения с {{service}}», «{{target}} посещения при {{barber}}», «{{target}} посещения в {{shop}}», «{{target}} посещения в ритъм» («поредни периода» is **banned**: it reads as a streak), «{{years}} години с нас». The family sentence (`BadgeSeries.requirement`, §3.5 DC) drops the figure: «Посещения с фейд».

**Seasons**: `season-2026-summer` (2026-06-01 → 08-31, ended, announced 05-01), `season-2026-autumn` (09-01 → 11-30, open, announced 08-01), `event-2026-beard-week` (09-22 → 10-05, open, announced 08-22). The projector moves every window by whole days relative to the shop calendar's today, as the day fixtures do, so any run shows one open season, one open event and one ended season.

**Tiers, rewards, rhythm**: model.ts `examples.ts` values: tiers Бронз 0 · Сребро 6 · Злато 12 · Платина 20 (365 days, completed visits); rewards `reward-beard` «Безплатно оформяне на брада» 120 т., `reward-cut` «Безплатно подстригване» 250 т., `reward-product` «Продукт по избор» 400 т. (NEW, to show a locked row); streak `cadence` rollingDays 42, grace 7. Punch rule `rule-every-10th-visit` («Безплатно подстригване», size 10); NEW coupon `coupon-punch-free-cut` (`free_service`, exclusive) and Георги's held grant `grant-dev-georgi-punch` (his 20th stamp).

**Expected progress, derived** (≈: the projector computes the exact values; the seed spec asserts invariants, §7.5). Георги's inputs (`tools/seed/fixtures.mjs:215-235`, `:506-512`): 13 visits in the last year at 26–28-day gaps, 10 older ones at 21–24-day gaps starting ≈369 days back, and today's `appt-dev-1`, **completed**: 24 worked visits, all with a fade (1 in 4 with a beard), all with Ivan, all at 10:00, ≈30 % booked online.

## 5. Component specifications (D8)

### 5.0 Placement and the dependency direction

| Piece                                                                                          | Kind         | Lib / folder                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Imports (checked against "patterns never import controls")                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ui-sheet-headline` media slot, `uiMediaTone`, the exit fade                                   | EXTENDED     | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/sheet-headline/`                                                                                                                                                                                                   | none new; the media is **projected** (`[uiHeadlineMedia]`), never imported                               |
| collapse cross-fade                                                                            | EXTENDED CSS | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/sheet-header/sheet-header.css`, `libs/ui/controls/src/lib/modal-sheet/modal-sheet.css`                                                                                                                             | —                                                                                                        |

| `ui-metric`                                                                                    | NEW          | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/metric/`                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `UiIcon` ✓                                                                                               |
| `UiMark`, `ui-mark-scale`                                                                      | NEW          | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/mark-scale/`                                                                                                                                                                                                       | none; `ui-slider` (controls) imports it ✓ (controls → patterns is the legal direction)                   |
| `ui-gauge`                                                                                     | NEW          | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/gauge/`                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `UiIcon`, `UiMarkScale` ✓                                                                                |
| `ui-streak-track`                                                                              | NEW          | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/streak-track/`                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `UiIcon`, `UiVisuallyHiddenDirective` (modifiers) ✓                                                      |
| `ui-medal`, `button[uiMedal]`                                                                  | NEW          | `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/medal/`                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `UiIcon`, `UiGauge` (pattern → pattern) ✓; the «Ново» badge projected                                    |

| tokens `--sys-alpha-tint`, `--sys-stroke-mark`, `--sys-stroke-emphasis`, `--control-size-tile` | NEW          | `libs/ui/tokens/tokens.css`                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | —                                                                                                        |
| feature bodies                                                                                 | NEW lib      | `libs/features/staff/client-card` (`@creativo/features/staff/client-card`, project `staff-client-card`, tags `scope:app`, `type:feature`, prefix `lib`: the `staff-dashboard` / `client-appointments` precedent; `shared-catalog` uses `cr`) | → ui, application, shared/visits ✓ (no shared/catalog import: the catalogue arrives as a lookup, §5.2.1) |
| visits list, row, filter, the moved event head                                                 | NEW lib      | `libs/features/shared/visits` (`@creativo/features/shared/visits`, project `shared-visits`, tags `scope:app`, `type:feature`, prefix `lib`)                                                                                                  | → ui, application ✓                                                                                      |

Graph (no cycles): `staff/dashboard → staff/client-card → shared/visits → ui/*`; `staff/dashboard → shared/visits` (the moved event head); `client/appointments → shared/visits`. The showcase (`scope:showcase`) imports only `libs/ui/*`, so the DS pieces get showcase pages and the feature bodies are demoed in the app.

Conventions every piece obeys (old spec §7.0.3, kept): `ViewEncapsulation.None`, `OnPush`, host class `ui-<name>`, state as `data-*` on the host, signal inputs with the `ui` prefix and SwiftUI names, CSS in `@layer sys-components` with tokens only (no hex, no `rgb()`), `font:` shorthands followed by `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums`, reduced-motion branches, consumers own `data-testid`, a Vitest `*.spec.ts` beside each piece, a showcase page `apps/showcase/src/app/pages/controls/<slug>/<slug>.page.{ts,html,css}`. **Input vocabulary** (house, 18 DS components): `uiLabel` is always the **accessible name**; a visible small line is `uiCaption`; an image is `uiSrc`; a progress value is `uiValue` / `uiValueText`; sizes are `uiControlSize` on the 36/44/52 ladder (`small`/`regular`/`large`, `--control-size-*`) and never redefine a rung.

### 5.1 Design-system pieces

#### 5.1.1 `ui-sheet-headline`: the trailing media slot (EXTENDED; supersedes old §7.1.1)

```ts
export type UiHeadlineMediaTone = 'warning';

/** The head's face (≙ a Contacts card photo). Exactly one per headline, a `large` (52) control. */
@Directive({
  selector: '[uiHeadlineMedia]',
  host: { class: 'ui-headline-media' },
})
export class UiHeadlineMedia {}

@Component({
  selector: 'ui-sheet-headline',
  template: ` <div class="ui-sheet-headline__body">
    <div class="ui-sheet-headline__text"><ng-content /></div>
    <span class="ui-sheet-headline__media" aria-hidden="true"
      ><ng-content select="[uiHeadlineMedia]"
    /></span>
  </div>`,
  host: {
    class: 'ui-sheet-headline',
    '[attr.data-folded]': 'uiFolded() ? "" : null',
    '[attr.data-media]': 'media() ? "" : null',
    '[attr.data-media-tone]': 'media() ? uiMediaTone() : null',
  },
})
export class UiSheetHeadline {
  readonly uiFolded = input(false, { transform: booleanAttribute }); // unchanged
  readonly uiMediaTone = input<UiHeadlineMediaTone | null>(null); // NEW
  protected readonly media = contentChild(UiHeadlineMedia); // NEW
}
/** Import bundle: `contentChild` matches only an imported directive. */
export const UI_SHEET_HEADLINE = [UiSheetHeadline, UiHeadlineMedia] as const;
```

| Aspect       | Spec                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| No media     | `.ui-sheet-headline:not([data-media]) .ui-sheet-headline__media { display: none }`; `__text` takes today's body recipe (flex column, `gap: var(--sys-space-tight)`, children `margin: 0`). **Pixel-identical to today** (spec on the existing consumers' markup)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Media        | `[data-media] .ui-sheet-headline__body { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; column-gap: var(--sys-space-regular); align-items: start }` and `[data-media] .ui-sheet-headline__text { min-block-size: var(--control-size-large); justify-content: center }`. The text column has the disc's height as its floor and centres in it: a one-line title + one line (24 × 1.1 + 4 + 13 × 1.4 = 48.6 pt) sits balanced beside the 52 disc; a longer title grows downward while the disc stays on its first line ([Revolut Business](https://mobbin.com/screens/473ee23d-dda1-49b8-9770-8386494eba4d)). CSS only: no feature measures text. The leading edge stays every other page's edge |
| Tone         | `[data-media-tone='warning'] .ui-sheet-headline__media::after`: absolute, `inset: 0`, `border-radius: var(--control-radius-capsule)`, `box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 var(--sys-stroke-emphasis) var(--sys-color-warning), inset 0 0 0 calc(var(--sys-stroke-emphasis) * 2) var(--ui-sheet-headline-media-gap, var(--sys-color-surface))`. Inset, because `__body` is `overflow: clip`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Exit fade    | the media rides the large title's exit fade (§5.1.2), so the disc never ghosts behind the bar's chips                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Clip rule    | every new wrapper uses `overflow: clip`, never `hidden`/`auto` (a scroll container would steal the title's view-timeline)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Fold         | media folds with the body; `uiFolded` and the view-timeline rule unchanged                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| A11y         | media `aria-hidden` (the `h2` and the line say it in words); the ring is decoration                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Custom props | `--ui-sheet-headline-gap` (existing), `--ui-sheet-headline-media-gap` (NEW)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Spec         | `sheet-headline.spec.ts`: no-media render unchanged; `UI_SHEET_HEADLINE` + `ui-avatar uiHeadlineMedia` → `data-media`, grid, trailing order, the 52 floor; `uiMediaTone="warning"` → `data-media-tone`; a lone `UiSheetHeadline` import projects without `data-media` (documents the bundle rule)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Showcase     | NEW page `sheet-headline` (plain; with an avatar; paused ring; a two-line name; with a medal)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Must not     | accept more than one media node; draw the ring outset; put the media before the title                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

#### 5.1.2 The collapse: one timeline, a cross-fade (EXTENDED CSS, every sheet page)

Today (`app-capture.md` §2): the fade flips on with `data-collapsed` when the title's bottom passes the **bar's bottom** (≈60 px), but the compact title's reveal runs `exit 20% → exit 100%` of a view-timeline measured against the **scroller's top** (≈40 → ≈120 px). From ≈40 to ≈110 px the big name ghosts behind the translucent chips under an empty bar (`light-53-search-collapse-strip.png`). Aligning the two ranges alone would still show **two names** at once (at exit 50 % the compact title is half in while half the large title shows through the light fade). iOS avoids that because the large title is gone before the inline one appears. So: one timeline, measured from the bar's bottom edge, driving a cross-fade.

```css
/* sheet-header.css — the sentinel's timeline starts under the bar (CHANGED) */
[uisheetlargetitle] {
  view-timeline-name: --ui-sheet-headline; /* unchanged */
  view-timeline-inset: var(
      --modal-sheet-chrome-height,
      var(--control-size-large)
    )
    auto; /* NEW */
}
@supports (animation-timeline: view()) {
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
    /* the large title (and the headline's media) leave over the first half… (NEW) */
    [uisheetlargetitle],
    .ui-sheet-headline[data-media] .ui-sheet-headline__media {
      animation: ui-sheet-headline-out linear both;
      animation-timeline: --ui-sheet-headline;
      animation-range: exit 0% exit 50%;
    }
    /* …the compact title arrives over the second half (was exit 20% → 100%) */
    .ui-sheet-header > [uititle] {
      animation-range: exit 50% exit 100%;
    }
    .ui-sheet-header {
      animation-range: exit 0% exit 100%;
    } /* the non-modal landed bar */
  }
}
@keyframes ui-sheet-headline-out {
  from {
    opacity: 1;
  }
  to {
    opacity: 0;
  }
}

/* modal-sheet.css — the top fade rides the same timeline across the whole exit (NEW) */
@supports (animation-timeline: view()) {
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference) {
    .modal-sheet__chrome::before {
      animation: ui-modal-sheet-chrome-fade linear both;
      animation-timeline: --ui-sheet-headline;
      animation-range: exit 0% exit 100%;
    }
  }
}
@keyframes ui-modal-sheet-chrome-fade {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}
```

- With the inset, `exit 0%` is the title's top reaching the bar's bottom and `exit 100%` its bottom reaching it, exactly where the sentinel stamps `data-collapsed` (`sheet-header.ts`: rootMargin minus the header height). At every scroll position at most one name is visible, and the fade is full the moment the compact title is.
- `--modal-sheet-chrome-height` is already published on the scroller by `ui-modal-sheet` (`modal-sheet.ts:147-167`); outside the modal shell the fallback is the 52 px bar. The timeline's scope is the existing `.ui-sheet__surface:has(.ui-sheet-header) { timeline-scope: --ui-sheet-headline }`, which already covers the headline and the chrome.
- The state line is not faded: it passes under the bar after the fade is full.
- Where the timeline is inactive (`titleAlwaysVisible`, a folded headline, no scroll-driven animations, reduced motion), the animations are inert and today's binary rules decide.
- **`titleAlwaysVisible` shows the TITLE from rest, never the fade** (wave D r5, 2026-09-28). The shell used to force the chrome's fade on for `[data-title-always]`, so on the photo page (§3.7) the fade's 3rem overhang veiled the full-bleed picture's top 30 pt with nothing scrolled (and the ladder's first card's top edge). The rule is gone: an always-titled bar's fade rises with the header's own no-sentinel collapse (any scroll at all, `sheet-header.ts`), as every other page's rises with its content. No new input: no consumer wants a fade over content that has not moved (the country picker, the ladder, the block sheet's root, the photo viewer). `modal-sheet.spec.ts` pins it; `client-card-glance.spec.ts` checks the photo page at rest (`0|clear`: fade off, or the hero at or below the fade's bottom).
- **Scope.** This changes every `ui-modal-sheet`: the staff visit sheet's pages, the block editor, `ui-detail-sheet`, and the client app's visit sheet (`appointments-screenshots.spec.ts` baselines).
- **Spec:** `sheet-header.spec.ts` and `modal-sheet.spec.ts` gain CSS-contract cases (the inset, the three ranges, both keyframes present). **Verification** (wave D, Playwright at 390×844, both themes) on four sheets: the staff client glance, the ladder's add-client search, the block editor, and the client app's visit sheet: at scrollTop 0…140 step 10, `largeTitleOpacity × compactTitleOpacity < 0.1`, and the compact title's opacity is 1 wherever the chrome fade is ≥ 0.95.

#### 5.1.3 `ui-section-header`: a sheet-sized heading (EXTENDED)

```ts
export type UiSectionHeaderProminence = 'standard' | 'increased'; // NEW · SwiftUI's own two values
export class UiSectionHeader {
  readonly uiAlignment = input<UiSectionHeaderAlign>('leading'); // unchanged
  readonly uiOnMedia = input(false); // unchanged
  readonly uiEyebrowTone = input<UiSectionHeaderEyebrowTone>('accent'); // unchanged
  /** ≙ `.headerProminence`. null (default) = today's landing trio, unchanged. (NEW) */
  readonly uiHeaderProminence = input<UiSectionHeaderProminence | null>(null);
  /** Leading glyph, `increased` only. (NEW) */
  readonly uiIcon = input<UiIconName | null>(null);
  /** `standard` only: sticks under the sheet bar (≙ a plain List's pinned section header). (NEW) */
  readonly uiPinned = input(false);
}
// slots: [uiEyebrow] [uiTitle] [uiLede] (landing) · [uiTitle] [uiSectionDetail] [uiSectionAction] (increased, standard)
```

| Aspect     | `increased` (a module heading in a sheet)                                                                                                                                                                  | `standard` (a list-section label)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Layout     | grid `auto minmax(0,1fr) auto` (glyph · title + detail · action), `align-items: center`, `column-gap: var(--sys-space-compact)`, `min-block-size: var(--control-size-regular)` when an action is projected | one line: title + detail, `padding-inline: var(--ui-section-header-inset, 0)`: **flush with the group's outer edge**, the edge the sheet's existing group labels already use (`staff-visit-editor.html:129-131`)                                                                                                    |
| Type       | title `--sys-font-headline` (18/600); detail `--sys-font-subheadline`                                                                                                                                      | title and detail `--sys-font-footnote`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Ink        | title foreground; glyph `--sys-color-secondary-label` at 20 px; detail secondary                                                                                                                           | secondary; sentence case (no eyebrow, no uppercase)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Action     | the consumer projects `button[uiSectionAction] uiButton uiButtonStyle="plain" uiControlSize="regular"` with a trailing `nav.disclosure`; hugged trailing                                                   | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Pinned     | —                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `position: sticky; inset-block-start: var(--modal-sheet-chrome-height, 0); background: var(--sys-color-surface); z-index: 1`                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| A11y       | the consumer's `h3 uiTitle`; the action names its destination                                                                                                                                              | the `h3` labels the list below (`aria-labelledby`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Migrations | —                                                                                                                                                                                                          | the sheet's hand-rolled group labels (`span uiText uiFont="footnote" uiForegroundStyle="secondary"`, `staff-visit-editor.html:129-131` and siblings) move to `standard` in WP7; the client list's `.appointments__section-head` (`client-appointments.html:273-281`) is replaced by `lib-visit-list`'s heads in WP4 |
| Spec       | `section-header.spec.ts` (NEW file: the component has none): default render unchanged; `increased` places glyph, title, action; `standard` one line; `uiPinned` stamps `data-pinned`                       | (same file)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Showcase   | the existing `section-header` page gains three demos                                                                                                                                                       | (same page)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Must not   | uppercase or track a sheet heading; accept two actions                                                                                                                                                     | render a glyph                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

#### 5.1.4 `ui-scroll-row`: shelves inside a sheet (EXTENDED)

```ts
export class UiScrollRow {
  readonly uiSnap = input<UiScrollRowSnap>('start'); // unchanged
  readonly uiSpacing = input<UiSpacing>('regular'); // unchanged
  /** `true` = the viewport (today); `'container'` = cancel the parent's inline padding (a sheet column). (CHANGED type) */
  readonly uiFullBleed = input<
    boolean | 'container',
    boolean | '' | 'container'
  >(false, {
    transform: (value) => (value === '' ? true : value),
  });
  /** ≙ `containerRelativeFrame(.horizontal, count:, span:)`: item width as a share of the row. (NEW) */
  readonly uiContainerRelativeCount = input<number | null>(null);
  readonly uiContainerRelativeSpan = input(1);
}
// host: data-full-bleed = '' | 'container' | null · data-relative · style --ui-scroll-row-count, --ui-scroll-row-span
```

CSS: the existing presence selector `.ui-scroll-row[data-full-bleed]` (`scroll-row.css:58`) becomes `[data-full-bleed='']`, so `'container'` never also gets the viewport bleed. Every `data-spacing` rule also sets `--ui-scroll-row-gap`. `[data-full-bleed='container']` → `margin-inline: calc(-1 * var(--ui-scroll-row-gutter, var(--ui-sheet-inset, var(--sys-space-regular))))`, matched by `padding-inline` and `scroll-padding-inline`. `[data-relative] > *` → `inline-size: calc((100% - (var(--ui-scroll-row-count) - 1) * var(--ui-scroll-row-gap)) / var(--ui-scroll-row-count) * var(--ui-scroll-row-span) + (var(--ui-scroll-row-span) - 1) * var(--ui-scroll-row-gap))`. Peek comes from a non-integer `count / span` (7 / 2 = 3.5 visible). Spec: existing cases unchanged; both bleeds exclusive; the width formula. Showcase: the `scroll-row` page gains "In a sheet" and "Peek 3.5".

#### 5.1.5 `uiListRow` `uiRole` (EXTENDED; promotes the house exits recipe)

`readonly uiRole = input<'destructive' | null>(null)` on `UiListRow` (`libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/list-row/list-row.ts`), the same name `uiButton` already uses (≙ `Button(role: .destructive)`); host `data-role`. CSS: `.ui-list-row[data-role='destructive'] { color: var(--sys-color-destructive-label) }` (the `-label` lift, legible on the segment: `theme-light.css:55`, `theme-dark.css:58`), the leading glyph inherits; `.ui-list-row[data-role='destructive'][data-armed] { font-weight: 600 }` (the consumer stamps `data-armed`, the block sheet's confirm arm). This is the recipe that lives today in dashboard feature CSS (`staff-visit-editor.sections.css:322-336`), unreachable from the new lib. Migrations (WP7): the visit editor's exits (`staff-visit-editor.html:2510-2533`) and the block editor's (`staff-block-editor.html:657-708`) switch to `uiRole="destructive"`, and the `.staff-visit__exit--destructive` rules are deleted. Spec: `list-row.spec.ts` gains the role and armed cases. Showcase: the `list-row` page gains a destructive row.

**`uiTrailingWraps` (EXTENDED, WP7 review r6)** ≙ LabeledContent's VALUE in a Form: a value row — a one-word label beside the long part («Приложени» · «Безплатно подстригване (17,50 €)») — where the default (label ellipsized, trailing run never shrinking) cut the label to «Пр…». `readonly uiTrailingWraps = input(false, { transform: booleanAttribute })`, host `data-trailing-wraps`. CSS (list-row.css, after the default `[uitrailing] { flex-shrink: 0 }`): the run lays its parts on one line at the tight gap and shrinks to `max-inline-size: 60%` (`flex-shrink: 1; min-inline-size: 0; text-align: end`); its text (every child but `.ui-icon`, or a bare-text run itself) wraps balanced at the trailing edge, `-webkit-line-clamp: 2` (a 330 phone never shows a third line); the glyph never shrinks. Bound on the visit sheet's four value rows («Приложени», «Отстъпка», «Бакшиш», «Салон») and the block sheet's «Повтаряне»; the feature's `.staff-visit__row-value` layout rules (and their `.ui-icon` reach-in) are deleted. Measured live (Георги): «Приложени»/«Отстъпка» label whole at 330/390/1024; the value 1 line at 1024, 2 at 390, 2 clamped at 330 («Безплатно / подстригване», the amount on the dock's struck price). Engine note: when the clamp falls exactly on a line break, Chromium paints no ellipsis for end-aligned text (it appends the «…» past the aligned end); mid-line cuts show it. Spec: `list-row.spec.ts` «lets a value row's trailing run give way». Showcase: «A figure, and words that wrap» gains two value rows.

#### 5.1.6 `span[uiBadge]`: leading glyph and on-media (EXTENDED; old §7.1.2 adopted)

- `readonly uiIcon = input<UiIconName | null>(null)`: the glyph leads, `aria-hidden`, inherits the caption size and the tone ink. A badge without it renders pixel-identically. `cr-capsule-list` (`libs/features/shared/catalog/src/lib/service-sheet/capsule-list.component.ts:12`, `:42`), which today projects `<ui-icon>` into the badge by hand, migrates to `uiIcon` in the same package: one grammar for a glyph on a capsule.
- `readonly uiOnMedia = input(false)`: a **label** over media, the white capsule in the caption face, read from the same white-on-media pair `uiButton uiOnMedia` uses (`button.css`). It exists so the DS separates "label over media" from "control over media": the photo rail's date is a label, never a button look on a non-control (a second, smaller target inside a tappable tile).
- The badge's four 14 % wash literals (`badge.css:53-95`) move to `--sys-alpha-tint` (§5.1.15), pixel-identical.
- Spec: glyph, on-media, the token (computed style unchanged). Showcase: the `badge` page gains both.

#### 5.1.7 `ui-quick-actions` + `a[uiQuickAction]`, `button[uiQuickAction]` (NEW; supersedes old §7.2)

```ts
@Component({
  selector: 'ui-quick-actions',
  template: '<ng-content />',
  host: {
    class: 'ui-quick-actions',
    role: 'group',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'uiLabel()',
    '[style.--ui-quick-actions-columns]': 'uiColumns()',
  },
})
export class UiQuickActions {
  readonly uiLabel = input.required<string>(); // «Връзка» (the accessible name)
  readonly uiColumns = input(4); // the consumer passes the present action count
}
@Component({
  selector: 'a[uiQuickAction], button[uiQuickAction]',
  imports: [UiIcon],
  template: `<ui-icon
      class="ui-quick-action__glyph"
      [uiName]="uiIcon()"
      uiScale="large"
      aria-hidden="true"
    />
    <span class="ui-quick-action__label"><ng-content /></span>`,
  host: { class: 'ui-quick-action', 'data-interactive': '' },
})
export class UiQuickAction {
  readonly uiIcon = input.required<UiIconName>();
}
```

| Aspect          | Spec                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Anatomy         | Apple Contacts / Telegram tiles ([Telegram](https://mobbin.com/screens/19fa286e-c101-4f24-8aa1-2b3c737fc3bb)): glyph over label in one translucent tile                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Grid            | `grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--ui-quick-actions-columns), minmax(0, 1fr))`, `gap: var(--sys-space-compact)`. Absent actions leave no hole                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Tile face       | **shared with `ui-choice-tiles`**: `min-block-size: var(--control-size-tile)` (64, NEW token), `border-radius: var(--control-radius-regular)`, fill `--sys-color-fill` (translucent control fill, house ruling). `choice-tiles.css:16` (`grid-auto-rows: calc(var(--sys-space-unit) * 16)`) moves to the same token in the same package (its radius and fill at `:61-62` already match), so the two faces cannot drift |
| Label           | `--sys-font-footnote` 500, foreground; up to **two lines**, `text-wrap: balance`, then ellipsis: the migrated client verbs row has five items («В календара», «Пак същото», «Премести», «Навигация», «Обади се», `client-appointments.html:507-580`) at ≈65 px each on a 330 px phone, where one line would cut two of them; the tile grows to fit                                                                     |
| Ink             | glyph 24 and label foreground; no accent (one accent per page; the ticket owns it)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| States          | the `[data-interactive]` state layer (hover + the ungated `:active` mirror, `--sys-press-scale-surface`), focus ring on the tile                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| A11y            | `role="group"` named by `uiLabel`; each item a native link or button; the consumer's `aria-label` begins with the visible word (WCAG 2.5.3)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Migration       | the client visit sheet's `.visit-actions` run (`client-appointments.css:424-480`) moves to this component in WP4; its bespoke 16 %/26 %/32 % accent washes are deleted (owner question Q4)                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Spec / showcase | `quick-actions.spec.ts` (4 and 5 columns, a two-line label); NEW page `quick-actions`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Must not        | dim or disable an item (absent instead); scroll; carry a destructive verb; show a busy state                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

#### 5.1.8 `ui-notice` (NEW; old §7.7 adopted)

Selector `ui-notice`; inputs `uiTone: 'neutral' | 'accent' | 'warning' | 'destructive'`, `uiIcon`, `uiAnnounce: 'off' | 'polite' | 'assertive'`, `uiLabel` (the accessible name when there is no projected title); slots `[uiNoticeTitle]`, default (facts), `[uiNoticeActions]`. Surface, compact form, tone rules, a11y (`role="note"`) and "must not" as old §7.7, with `--sys-alpha-tint` (§5.1.15) as its wash. It is the one retry grammar in the stack (module and page failures alike). Spec `notice.spec.ts`; NEW showcase page `notice`.

#### 5.1.9 `ui-metric` (NEW; old §7.3 with one delta)

Selector `ui-metric`; inputs `uiValue` (pre-formatted, `null` → «—»), `uiUnit`, `uiCaption` (the **visible** caption: «за размяна», «интервал»), `uiCaptionHidden`, `uiIcon`, `uiDelta`, `uiTrend`, `uiCountFrom`, `uiLabel` (the accessible name; defaults to value + unit + caption), `uiControlSize: 'regular' | 'large'`, `uiAlignment: 'leading' | 'center'`; slot `[uiMetricAccessory]`; all `span`s (phrasing content, so it sits inside `button[uiCard]`); `role="group"`. Types: regular = value `title3`, unit `subheadline`, caption `caption`; large = value `title`, unit `callout`, caption `footnote`. The value never takes a sentiment colour. Spec `metric.spec.ts`; NEW showcase page `metric`.

#### 5.1.10 `UiMark` + `ui-mark-scale` (NEW; one mark grammar for the slider and the gauge)

```ts
/** A point on a scale that matters: a threshold, a tier, a level. */
export interface UiMark {
  readonly value: number;
  readonly label: string | null; // under the point; null = a notch without words
  readonly short?: string | null; // inside a `steps` node: «I», «II»
  readonly detail?: readonly string[]; // ≤2 caption lines under the label: «3.06», «+50 т.», «още 6»
}
@Component({
  selector: 'ui-mark-scale',
  host: { class: 'ui-mark-scale', 'aria-hidden': 'true' },
})
export class UiMarkScale {
  readonly uiMarks = input.required<readonly UiMark[]>();
  readonly uiMin = input(0);
  readonly uiMax = input(1);
  /** A label closer than this (as a share of the range) to the previous shown one is dropped. */
  readonly uiMinGap = input(0.12);
}
export function visibleMarks(
  marks: readonly UiMark[],
  min: number,
  max: number,
  minGap: number,
): readonly UiMark[]; // pure, specced
```

Placement is CSS only: each label sits at its ratio `r` and translates by `calc(r * -100%)` of its own width, so a label at the start is start-aligned, at the end end-aligned, in the middle centred: it never hangs past the track. `--ui-mark-scale-inset` lets the slider keep its thumb/2 inset. `ui-slider` (`libs/ui/controls/src/lib/slider/slider.ts:11-14`) keeps its public name as `export type UiSliderMark = UiMark & { readonly label: string }` and replaces its `.ui-slider__marks` block with `<ui-mark-scale>`: its end labels stop overhanging the track (a visible change, listed for the slider's showcase and the discount page's tip tiles). Dropped labels stay in the owner's `aria-valuetext`. Spec `mark-scale.spec.ts` (`visibleMarks`; the ratio custom property); the slider's spec keeps its cases. NEW showcase page `mark-scale`.

#### 5.1.11 `ui-gauge`: the meter (NEW; old §7.4 with deltas)

```ts
export type UiGaugeStyle = 'linearCapacity' | 'discreteCapacity' | 'steps'; // `steps` ≙ a custom GaugeStyle (NEW)
export type UiGaugeControlSize = 'small' | 'regular'; // local union (patterns cannot import UiControlSize)

export class UiGauge {
  readonly uiValue = input.required({ transform: numberAttribute });
  readonly uiMin = input(0, { transform: numberAttribute }); // the house ui-slider's names
  readonly uiMax = input(1, { transform: numberAttribute });
  readonly uiGaugeStyle = input<UiGaugeStyle>('linearCapacity');
  readonly uiControlSize = input<UiGaugeControlSize>('regular');
  readonly uiMarks = input<readonly UiMark[]>([]); // thresholds · slot captions · step nodes
  readonly uiColumns = input<number | null>(null); // discrete slots per row (5 → a 5×2 card)
  readonly uiRewardIcon = input<UiIconName | null>(null); // the last discrete slot
  readonly uiLabel = input.required<string>(); // accessible name
  readonly uiValueText = input.required<string>();
}
// host: role=meter · aria-valuemin/max/now (clamped) · aria-label · aria-valuetext · data-gauge-style ·
//       data-control-size · data-complete · data-empty · style --ui-gauge-value, --ui-gauge-slots, --ui-gauge-columns
```

| Aspect             | Spec                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Tint               | `--ui-gauge-tint`, default **`--sys-color-accent`**: the fill every meter already uses (`progress-ring.css:26`, `progress-view.css:12`/`:45`, `slider.css:7`). WCAG 1.4.11 is met the house way: the value is always printed as text beside the meter (a figure, a fraction, a sentence), and the track is `--sys-color-fill` on the card; the gauge never carries information only its colour holds (§5.1.15)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| linearCapacity     | track `--sys-color-fill`, fill to `(value − min)/(max − min)`; marks draw a notch across the track (`--sys-stroke-emphasis` wide, `--sys-color-surface-secondary`: a cut, readable on fill and track); labelled marks are placed by `ui-mark-scale` under the track (`regular` only)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| discreteCapacity   | `uiColumns` → `grid-template-columns: repeat(var(--ui-gauge-columns), minmax(0, 1.5rem))`; empty slot ring `--sys-stroke-mark` in `--sys-color-secondary-label`; the reward slot always last, and at `regular` it never drops its 16 glyph: its disc alone keeps the 20 rung (`max(100%, var(--ui-icon-medium))`, centred on the row), overhanging a narrower column by a point each way (wave D r4: five slots in a 330 half tile are ≈ 18); ≤12 slots (above → linear + a dev warning)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| steps (NEW)        | a node track: one node per mark, **evenly spaced** (steps are ranks, not distances); node disc `calc(var(--sys-space-unit) * 6)` (24) showing `short` in `--sys-font-caption` 600. **States, strongest first (wave D r4, DV14):** reached nodes (value ≥ mark.value) are the solid tint; a level PASSED (below the current one) draws the registry check `checklist.done` at 16 in `--sys-color-on-accent` (a mark, 3.04:1 ≥ 3:1) and prints no numeral, since white type on the tint is 3.04:1, under AA at 11 px; the highest reached node (the current one) wears a `--sys-stroke-emphasis` tint ring inside its box, with its numeral in `--sys-color-accent-label` on the tint's wash (a blank node, as on a tier track, is a solid dot in the ring); the next node and those ahead are `--sys-color-fill` ringed at `--sys-stroke-mark` in the secondary ink, never the tint, the next one's numeral in the primary ink and the rest in `--sys-color-secondary-label-on-fill`. Accent strokes mark what is earned only. The link between the last reached and the next node fills proportionally `(value − prev)/(next − prev)`; earlier links full, later links track. `label` under each node (caption), `detail` lines under it (caption secondary). Serves the tier track (LH), the season track (AF, CE) and the medal ladder (DD): one grammar for three uses |
| Kept from old §7.4 | whole slots only, no mount animation, `M15`/`M10`/`M11` motion on real changes, presentational inside a button or an img, the "must not" list                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Spec / showcase    | `gauge.spec.ts` (each style; ratios; the steps' partial link; ARIA); NEW page `gauge` (points with notches, labelled catalogue, stamp card 5×2, tier steps, season steps, medal ladder, small)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

#### 5.1.12 `ui-streak-track` (NEW; old §7.5 with one delta)

Old §7.5 adopted (periods `UiStreakPeriod {id, mark, caption, description}`, `uiCount`, `uiCountLabel`, `uiLabel`, `uiProtectedIcon`, `uiFrozen`; `role="img"` + a visually hidden list; marks by shape and glyph; no countdown), plus `uiCountUnit` (the unit printed with the figure: «24 посещения», because the marks are periods and the figure counts visits), `uiCountWord` (a word in the figure's place — «Винаги» over «в ритъм» — where the owner prints the count elsewhere, wave D r6) and **`uiControlSize: 'small' | 'regular'`** (NEW). `small` = marks `calc(var(--sys-size-dot) * 2)` (16), no captions, figure `--sys-font-title3`, for the glance tile; `regular` = old §7.5 (1.5rem marks, captions, figure `title2`). Spec `streak-track.spec.ts`; NEW showcase page `streak-track`.

#### 5.1.13 `ui-medal`, `button[uiMedal]` (NEW; supersedes old §7.6)

```ts
export type UiMedalState = 'notStarted' | 'inProgress' | 'earned';
export type UiMedalControlSize = 'regular' | 'large'; // 44 · 52: the house ladder (local union)

@Component({
  selector: 'ui-medal, button[uiMedal]',
  imports: [UiIcon, UiGauge],
  host: {
    class: 'ui-medal',
    '[attr.role]': 'isButton ? null : "img"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'uiLabel()',
    '[attr.data-state]': 'uiState()',
    '[attr.data-control-size]': 'uiControlSize()',
    '[attr.data-interactive]': 'isButton ? "" : null',
  },
})
export class UiMedal {
  readonly uiState = input.required<UiMedalState>();
  readonly uiIcon = input<UiIconName | null>(null); // the registry emblem (loyalty.badge.*)
  readonly uiSrc = input<string | null>(null); // art: a barber's face, a service or shop cover, owner art; wins
  readonly uiMonogram = input<string | null>(null); // «Ф» · «И» · «Ц» when a service, barber or shop family has no art; wins over uiIcon
  readonly uiLevel = input<string | null>(null); // the rim numeral: the HIGHEST EARNED level only
  readonly uiTitle = input<string | null>(null); // under the disc; null = disc only
  readonly uiCaption = input<string | null>(null); // «24/25 до III» · «4.09» · «0/3 до I»
  readonly uiValue = input<number | null>(null); // 0…1, the thin meter (in progress, or earned with a next level)
  readonly uiValueText = input<string | null>(null); // required for the meter to draw
  readonly uiControlSize = input<UiMedalControlSize>('large');
  readonly uiLabel = input.required<string>(); // one sentence (below)
  protected readonly isButton =
    inject(ElementRef).nativeElement.tagName === 'BUTTON';
}
// slot: [uiMedalMarker] — top-trailing of the disc; consumers project span[uiBadge uiTone="accent"] «Ново» or «+2»
```

| Aspect           | Spec                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Sizes            | disc `regular` = `var(--control-size-regular)` (44: the glance strip, the season's families row); `large` = `var(--control-size-large)` (52: rails, grids, the detail's headline media). The ≥ 64 pt tap target of a rail or grid tile comes from the tile's padding and title, not a new rung. Glyph and monogram are **disc-derived**: `calc(var(--ui-medal-disc) * 0.5)`, the avatar fallback's precedent (`avatar.css:96-101`, `0.55 × disc`), so no off-ladder glyph size is minted |
| notStarted       | disc `--sys-color-fill`; the real emblem, monogram or art as a **grey silhouette** (`--sys-color-secondary-label`; art `filter: grayscale(1)` + `opacity: .45`); ring `--sys-stroke-mark` **dashed** secondary. Never a padlock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| inProgress       | disc `--sys-color-fill`; glyph foreground; ring `--sys-stroke-mark` solid `--ui-medal-tint`; the thin meter under the disc (`ui-gauge uiControlSize="small"`, the disc's width)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| earned           | disc filled `--ui-medal-tint` (default **`--sys-color-accent`**, the meter family, §5.1.15); glyph and monogram in **`--sys-color-on-accent`** (the pairing every accent-filled control already uses: `borderedProminent`); art keeps its colour inside a tint ring; a next level in progress keeps the thin meter                                                                                                                                                                       |
| The numeral rule | `uiLevel` is always the highest **earned** level and is absent when none is earned; a target appears only in words, in `uiCaption`; `uiTitle` never carries a numeral. Rim chip: `--sys-font-caption` 600, fill `--sys-color-surface`, ink foreground, ring `--sys-stroke-mark` `--sys-color-separator`                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Text             | title `--sys-font-footnote` 600, centred, `line-clamp: 2`; caption `--sys-font-caption` secondary; gap `--sys-space-tight`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Button form      | the state layer and ungated `:active` mirror, `--control-radius-prominent` focus ring on the whole tile                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| A11y             | static `role="img"`; the button form a native button; both named by one sentence carrying the state, the earned level, the caption in words and «ново» («Фейд, ниво II спечелено на 3 юни, 24 от 25 до ниво III, ново»); the inner gauge is presentational                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Motion           | M16 `medal.earn` (ring → disc cross-fade on a live change, `--sys-motion-duration-deliberate`); quiet on staff surfaces; reduced motion swaps                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Custom props     | `--ui-medal-tint`, `--ui-medal-disc` (read)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Spec / showcase  | `medal.spec.ts` (three states × two sizes; the numeral rule; src > monogram > icon; «Ново»); NEW page `medal`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Must not         | show a padlock or a percentage; differ by hue alone (ring vs disc, dashed vs solid); draw a progress ring; hide a requirement (a not-started medal always has its caption where it has a caption)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

#### 5.1.14 `ui-date-tile` (NEW; promoted from the client app's `.visit__date`)

```ts
export type UiDateTileTone = 'upcoming' | 'past' | 'hollow';
export type UiDateTileControlSize = 'regular' | 'large'; // 44 · 52 (`--control-size-regular` / `-large`)
@Component({
  selector: 'ui-date-tile',
  template: `<span class="ui-date-tile__caption">{{ uiCaption() }}</span>
    <span class="ui-date-tile__day">{{ uiDay() }}</span>`,
  host: {
    class: 'ui-date-tile',
    'aria-hidden': 'true',
    '[attr.data-tone]': 'uiTone()',
    '[attr.data-control-size]': 'uiControlSize()',
  },
})
export class UiDateTile {
  readonly uiDay = input.required<string | number>();
  /** The word over the day: whatever the section does not already say. «пт» under a month section; «септ.» under a year section. */
  readonly uiCaption = input.required<string>();
  readonly uiTone = input<UiDateTileTone>('past');
  readonly uiControlSize = input<UiDateTileControlSize>('regular');
}
```

Square, `--control-radius-subtle`; caption `--sys-font-caption`, day `--sys-font-headline` tabular. `upcoming` = the **existing** accent wash rung moved verbatim from `client-appointments.css` `.visit__date[data-upcoming]` (so `accent-label.spec.ts` measures the same text pairing), `--sys-color-accent-label` ink; `past` = `--sys-color-fill`, secondary ink; `hollow` (cancelled, no-show) = no fill, `inset 0 0 0 var(--sys-stroke-mark) var(--sys-color-separator)`, secondary ink. Decorative: the row's name carries the date. Why not `ui-date-badge` (`libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/date-badge`): that is a calendar grid's day (a number, a selection disc, an event dot), sized by its grid; a visit stamp is a captioned tile. Spec `date-tile.spec.ts`; NEW showcase page `date-tile`. `libs/ui/tokens/src/lib/accent-label.spec.ts` (`stampRung()` at :206, the path at :32) and `libs/ui/tokens/project.json` (test inputs :13, :24) switch to `libs/ui/patterns/src/lib/date-tile/date-tile.css`, selector `.ui-date-tile[data-tone='upcoming']`.

#### 5.1.15 Tokens and the one accent decision (NEW, `libs/ui/tokens/tokens.css`)

- `--sys-alpha-tint: 14%` (theme-independent): the one tone wash. `ui-notice` uses it and `badge.css`'s four 14 % literals move to it (§5.1.6), pixel-identical.
- `--sys-stroke-mark: 1.5px` (rings, dashed halos, empty slots) and `--sys-stroke-emphasis: 2px` (notches, the current node, the paused ring).
- `--control-size-tile: calc(var(--sys-space-unit) * 16)` (64): the tile face shared by `ui-quick-actions` and `ui-choice-tiles`.
- **One accent, one decision.** Meter and medal **fills** use `--sys-color-accent` (#f26b22), like the ring, the progress view, the slider and the prominent button. `--sys-color-accent-label` (#b04100 / #fb7735) stays the **text** ink (memory: "the fill stays #f26b22; accent-label is the text ink"), so the page never shows two oranges. Non-text contrast (WCAG 1.4.11): no gauge or medal carries information only its colour holds; the value is always text beside it, and states differ by form (filled disc, solid ring, dashed ring; filled node, ringed node). `accent-label.spec.ts` gains no fill pairings (it is the text-ink spec); `gauge.spec.ts` and `medal.spec.ts` assert their tint defaults resolve to `--sys-color-accent`.

#### 5.1.16 Reused unchanged, and what was considered and not used

Reused: `button[uiCouponCard]` (slots `[uiCouponGlyph]`, `[uiCouponBadge]`, `[uiCouponAction]`; the consumer marks it `uiInteractive`; EXTENDED in WP7 review r6: `uiValue` is `string | null` — `null` prints no figure line, the name alone, for a coupon whose name already says its rule; spec «prints no figure when the name already says the rule»), `ui-card` / `button[uiCard]` (`uiTone="muted"`), `ui-media-card` (`uiRatio`, `uiScrim`, `[uiCaption]`, `data-on-media`; `libs/ui/controls/src/lib/media-card/media-card.ts`), `button[uiListRow uiVariant="neutral"]` (the lone-row rung), `ui-grid`, `ui-flow`, `ui-stack`, `ui-scroll-column`, `ui-avatar`, `ui-choice-menu` (the filter), `ui-skeleton`, `ui-empty-state`, `ui-list-group` / `uiListRow`, `ui-sheet-action-bar`, `ui-modal-sheet`.

| Considered                                                        | Why not                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ui-detail-sheet` for the medal detail                            | a second modal sheet; D6 makes the detail a page inside the visit sheet (§3.5)                                                                                 |
| `ui-date-badge` for the visit stamp                               | a calendar grid's day, not a captioned stamp (§5.1.14)                                                                                                         |
| `ui-avatar-group` for the glance strip                            | it overlaps discs into one mark (`avatar-group.css`); the strip's three medals are three taps (D2.6)                                                           |
| an icon slot in `ui-empty-state` for the history's filtered empty | the component's doctrine refuses one ("No icon or illustration slot", `empty-state.ts`); the bare form + «Покажи всички» is what the client list already shows |
| a DS `uiHeadlineSize` input for the stack's title size            | the consumer already chooses the `h2`'s `uiFont` on every sheet page (six in the editor); `title2` on a head with a face is that choice, not a restyle         |

### 5.2 Feature bodies: `libs/features/staff/client-card` (NEW lib)

```
libs/features/staff/client-card/
  project.json · eslint.config.mjs · tsconfig.json · tsconfig.lib.json · tsconfig.spec.json · vite.config.mts · src/test-setup.ts
  src/index.ts
  src/lib/client-card.store.ts           ClientCardStore (@Injectable(), provided by the visit editor, resolved lazily)
  src/lib/client-card.contracts.ts       ClientCardSubject, ClientCardContext, ClientCardGrant, ClientCardCatalog, LoyaltyAnchor, AchievementScope
  src/lib/client-card.vm.ts              every VM type + Slice<T> (old §7.9)
  src/lib/client-card.presenter.ts       pure: head, capsules, actions, reward, visits module, loyalty tiles, strip, photos
  src/lib/loyalty.presenter.ts           pure: the «Лоялност» page
  src/lib/achievements.presenter.ts      pure: achievementsPageVm, scopePageVm, detailPageVm, medalVm, BADGE_EMBLEM_ICONS
  src/lib/history.presenter.ts           pure: stats cells, sections, paging line
  src/lib/client-card/client-card.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}                   lib-client-card
  src/lib/loyalty-tiles/loyalty-tiles.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}               lib-client-loyalty-tiles (F1–F3)
  src/lib/medal-strip/medal-strip.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}                   lib-client-medal-strip (G1)
  src/lib/client-loyalty/client-loyalty.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}             lib-client-loyalty
```
