```
  src/lib/client-history/client-history.{ts,html,css,spec.ts}             lib-client-history
```

Each component has its own stylesheet under the 10 kB budget; feature CSS lays out only (gaps, grid areas) and never restyles a DS part: the photo date is the badge's `uiOnMedia` in `ui-media-card`'s `[uiCaption]`, so there is **no overlay CSS** in the feature. Test ids: `client-*`, `rewards-*`, `achievements-*`, `history-*` (R17 row, top of file). Copy: `clientCard.*`, reused keys (§8.1).

#### 5.2.1 The store and the contracts

```ts
export interface ClientCardSubject {
  readonly id: string; // DraftClient.id
  readonly userId: string | null; // the account uid; null for a guest (ids starting `guest-`)
  readonly label: string;
  readonly phone: string | null;
  readonly phoneHref: string | null;
  readonly email: string | null;
  readonly avatarSrc: string | null;
}
/** One held reward, already worded by the editor's own discount formatter (no second formatter). */
export interface ClientCardGrant {
  readonly grantId: string;
  readonly title: string;
  readonly valueLabel: string;
  readonly expiresOn: string | null; // ISO date, or null
}
/** The catalogue as the card needs it: names and art, resolved by the dashboard (no catalogue service in the card). */
export interface ClientCardCatalog {
  serviceName(serviceId: string): string | null;
  serviceCoverSrc(serviceId: string): string | null;
  serviceCategory(
    serviceId: string,
  ): { readonly id: string; readonly name: string } | null;
  barberName(barberId: string): string | null;
  barberAvatarSrc(barberId: string): string | null;
  locationName(locationId: string): string | null;
  locationCoverSrc(locationId: string): string | null;
  readonly locationCount: number;
}
export interface ClientCardContext {
  readonly appointmentId: string | null; // the visit on screen
  readonly viewer: { readonly uid: string; readonly roles: readonly string[] };
  readonly isOwner: boolean; // the subject is the appointment's owner (§3.1.3)
  readonly settled: boolean;
  readonly partyOfOne: boolean;
  readonly legs: readonly {
    readonly serviceId: string;
    readonly variantId: string | null;
    readonly minutes: number;
    readonly barberId: string | null;
    readonly startsAtIso: string;
  }[];
  readonly grants: readonly ClientCardGrant[]; // [] unless isOwner; never one already on this bill
  readonly grantsOnBill: readonly ClientCardGrant[]; // [] unless isOwner; the owner's grants already on the draft bill — still held (DV5); E and LD draw them stamped where they stood (WP7 r6)
  readonly catalog: ClientCardCatalog;
}
export type LoyaltyAnchor = 'catalog' | 'stamps' | 'rhythm';
export type AchievementScope =
  | { readonly kind: 'category'; readonly categoryId: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'season'; readonly seasonId: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'archive' }
  | { readonly kind: 'day'; readonly day: string };
export type ClientCardRegion =
  'profile' | 'loyalty' | 'achievements' | 'visits' | 'photos' | 'history';

@Injectable()
export class ClientCardStore {
  open(subject: ClientCardSubject, context: ClientCardContext): void; // idempotent per subject id; a re-open refreshes
  readonly subject: Signal<ClientCardSubject | null>;
  readonly displayName: Signal<string | null>; // User name once read, else the label (bar title, photo page)
  readonly head: Signal<ClientHeadVm>; // renders from the snapshot at once
  readonly glance: Signal<ClientGlanceVm>; // every module's Slice; absent keys = absent modules
  readonly loyalty: Signal<Slice<ClientLoyaltyVm> | null>; // null = every loyalty flag off
  readonly achievementsData: Signal<Slice<AchievementsData> | null>; // catalogue + progress + season progress + account
  readonly history: Signal<ClientHistoryVm>; // paging and filter state included
  loadMoreHistory(): void;
  setHistoryFilter(filter: VisitFilter): void;
  retry(region: ClientCardRegion): void;
  photoById(photoId: string): ClientPhotoVm | null;
  barTitle(kind: string, subjectId: string | null): string | null; // scope and family titles
  readonly online: Signal<boolean>;
}
```

- **Scope pages compute, the store holds data.** `lib-client-achievements`, `lib-achievement-category` and `lib-achievement-detail` compute their VM in a `computed` from `store.achievementsData()` and their own inputs through the pure presenters (`achievementsPageVm(data, ctx)`, `scopePageVm(data, scope, ctx)`, `detailPageVm(data, seriesId, ctx)`): no per-call signal factories on the store, nothing re-created per change detection.
- **Ports it injects**, every one with `{ optional: true }` (a missing port = an absent slice): `PROFILE_PORT`, `AVATAR_UPLOADER`, `CLIENT_STATS_READER`, `WAITLIST_READER`, `CLIENT_HISTORY_READER` (NEW), `CLIENT_PHOTOS_READER` (NEW), `LOYALTY_READER` (NEW), `LOYALTY_CATALOG_READER` (NEW), `CLOCK`, `FEATURE_FLAGS`. It injects **no** catalogue service (`CatalogContentService` injects `CATALOG_READER` and `MEDIA_READER` non-optionally, `catalog-content.service.ts:40,52`): names and art arrive through `ClientCardContext.catalog`, and grants through `ClientCardContext.grants`. The visit rows are built by the pure `visitRowOf` (§5.3.2) with the same lookup.
- Flags are read once (`this.flags.isEnabled('loyalty.points')` etc.); a flag off makes its slice `null`. `users/{uid}` is read first (the D-9 gate), then the rest in parallel, each with a 10 s timeout that settles as failed. The catalogue is cached for the store's life. `online` follows `navigator.onLine` and its events; no "data from" time (the ports return `Result` values without cache metadata).

| Selector                   | Inputs                                     | Outputs                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Composes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Notes                                                     |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- |
| `lib-client-card`          | `titleId: string` (required)               | `openLoyalty: LoyaltyAnchor \| null`, `openAchievements: void`, `openMedal: string` (seriesId), `openHistory: void`, `openPhoto: string` (photoId), `openReward: string` (grantId), `removeReward: string` (grantId: an applied ticket's «Премахни», WP7 r6), `repeatLast: readonly VisitLeg[]`, `removeClient: void` | `UI_SHEET_HEADLINE`, `ui-avatar`, `span[uiBadge]`, `ui-flow`, `ui-notice`, `ui-quick-actions`, `button[uiCouponCard]`, `ui-section-header`, `lib-visit-row`, `ui-media-card`, `lib-client-loyalty-tiles`, `lib-client-medal-strip`, `ui-scroll-row`, `ui-card`, `ui-list-group`/`uiListRow` | renders the page's `h2`; host `data-testid="client-card"` |

VM names (in `client-card.vm.ts`; fields follow §3's region tables): `ClientHeadVm {name, stateLine, avatarSrc, initialsFrom, paused}`, `ClientCapsuleVm {id, text, tone, icon}` (named apart from `shared/catalog`'s `CapsuleVm`), `QuickActionVm {id, icon, label, href, accessibleName}`, `RewardReadyVm`, `ClientVisitsVm {last: VisitRowVm | null, next: readonly VisitRowVm[], waitlist: VisitRowVm | null, count}`, `LoyaltyTilesVm {points?, stamps?, rhythm?}`, `MedalVm {seriesId, state, icon, src, monogram, level, title, caption, value, valueText, isNew, accessibleName}`, `MedalStripVm {medals: readonly MedalVm[], cue: string}`, `ClientPhotoVm {photoId, appointmentId, url, takenAtIso, dateLabel, accessibleName, barberId, width, height}`, `ClientGlanceVm`, `ClientLoyaltyVm`, `AchievementsData`, `AchievementsPageVm`, `ScopePageVm`, `DetailPageVm`, `ClientHistoryVm {cells, facts, filter, sections: readonly VisitSectionVm[], paging}`.

### 5.3 The shared visits list: `libs/features/shared/visits` (NEW lib, D5)

#### 5.3.1 What moves out of `libs/features/client/appointments`, what stays

| Moves to `shared/visits`                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Stays client-only                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| the list view's row and section markup → `lib-visit-row`, `lib-visit-list`                                                                                                                                                             | the page chrome (back, view switch, «+»), the calendar view, the jump pills and their scroll spy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `groupVisitsByMonth`, `monthKeyOf`, `VisitSection` (from `visit-vm.ts`) → `visit-sections.ts` (`sectionVisits`, year-aware labels; fixes the two-«Юли» bug)                                                                            | `visit-vm.ts` itself, its type renamed **`ClientVisitVm`**: it still drives the calendar (`VisitDayVm`/`VisitMonthVm`, `byDay`), the open-visit sheet (`openVisit`), `addToCalendar`, `dayAriaLabel`, `fullDayLabel` (`client-appointments.ts:145-155, 309-341, 381-406, 533, 689, 726, 825, 837, 881, 934`); only the list view consumes `VisitRowVm` |
| the filter's union, predicate and menu (`client-appointments.html:70-124`, the `appointments.filter.*` keys) → `visit-filter.ts` + `lib-visit-filter` built on `ui-choice-menu`                                                        | `AppointmentsStore`, `visit-ics.ts`, the visit detail sheet with every verb, the cancel sheet                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| the date stamp CSS `.visit__date*` → DS `ui-date-tile`; `.visit__identity/.visit__with/.visit__face` → the row's own CSS (the face becomes a trailing `ui-avatar uiControlSize="small"`)                                               | `showVisit(id)`: now takes the id from `(openRow)` and looks the `ClientVisitVm` up in the page's existing map                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| the event head `libs/features/staff/dashboard/src/lib/shared/event-head/*` → `src/lib/event-head/*` (selector `lib-staff-event-head`, class `StaffEventHead`, the `.staff-event-head__*` class names and `hollowStatus` kept verbatim) | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

#### 5.3.2 View-model types, grouping, filter

```ts
export type VisitPerspective = 'client' | 'staff';
export type VisitGrain = 'month' | 'year';
export type VisitListStyle = 'insetGrouped' | 'plain'; // ≙ `.listStyle(…)`
export type VisitRowVariant = 'row' | 'card'; // a variant, not a size
export type VisitRowOutcome = 'open' | 'worked' | 'no_show' | 'cancelled'; // the SUBJECT's (staff), the root's (client)
export type VisitFilter =
  'all' | 'upcoming' | 'completed' | 'no_show' | 'cancelled';
export interface VisitRowMark {
  readonly icon: UiIconName;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly count: number | null;
}
export type VisitTrailing =
  | {
      readonly kind: 'amount';
      readonly text: string;
      readonly emphasis: 'primary' | 'secondary';
      readonly struck: boolean;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: 'status';
      readonly icon: UiIconName;
      readonly text: string;
      readonly tone: 'neutral' | 'warning';
    }
  | { readonly kind: 'badge'; readonly text: string } // «Този час»
  | {
      readonly kind: 'barber';
      readonly avatarSrc: string | null;
      readonly name: string;
      readonly several: boolean;
    } // client
  | null;
export interface VisitRowVm {
  readonly id: string; // the appointment id, or the waitlist request id
  readonly kind: 'visit' | 'waitlist';
  readonly startMs: number;
  readonly sectionKey: string;
  readonly upcoming: boolean;
  readonly tile: {
    readonly caption: string;
    readonly day: number;
    readonly tone: 'upcoming' | 'past' | 'hollow';
  } | null; // null: waitlist
  readonly leadingIcon: UiIconName | null; // waitlist: 'calendar.watching'
  readonly title: string;
  readonly struck: boolean;
  readonly lines: readonly StaffEventLine[]; // the event head's lines
  readonly quote: string | null; // the client's own words (booker == subject only)
  readonly trailing: VisitTrailing;
  readonly marks: readonly VisitRowMark[]; // staff: photos (count), discount
  readonly photos: readonly {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly url: string;
    readonly alt: string;
  }[]; // card variant, ≤3
  readonly outcome: VisitRowOutcome | null;
  readonly status: string | null;
  readonly accessibleName: string;
  readonly testId: string;
}
export interface VisitSectionVm {
  readonly key: string; // 'upcoming' | 'YYYY-MM' | 'YYYY'
  readonly label: string; // «Предстоящи» · «Септември 2026 г.» · «2026»
  readonly detail: string | null; // staff: worked visits in the section, absent at 0; client: null
  readonly rows: readonly VisitRowVm[];
  readonly testId: string;
}
export function sectionVisits(
  rows: readonly VisitRowVm[],
  options: {
    readonly grain: VisitGrain;
    readonly labels: {
      readonly upcoming: string;
      readonly section: (key: string) => string;
      readonly detail?: (rows: readonly VisitRowVm[]) => string | null;
    };
  },
): readonly VisitSectionVm[];
// upcoming first (soonest first), then sections newest first, rows newest first
export function matchesVisitFilter(
  row: VisitRowVm,
  filter: VisitFilter,
): boolean;
export interface VisitLookup {
  // the catalogue, as the row needs it
  serviceName(id: string): string | null;
  barberName(id: string): string | null;
  barberAvatarSrc(id: string): string | null;
  locationName(id: string): string | null;
  readonly locationCount: number;
}
export function visitRowOf(
  appointment: Appointment,
  options: {
    readonly perspective: VisitPerspective;
    readonly grain: VisitGrain;
    readonly subjectUserId: string | null;
    readonly currentAppointmentId: string | null;
    readonly nowMs: number;
    readonly locale: string;
    readonly photos?: readonly {
      readonly id: string;
      readonly url: string;
      readonly takenAtIso: string;
    }[];
    readonly photoCount?: number;
    readonly variant?: VisitRowVariant;
    readonly words: VisitWords;
  },
  lookup: VisitLookup,
): VisitRowVm; // pure: no DI, no NullInjectorError anywhere
export function waitlistRowOf(
  request: WaitlistRequest,
  options: { readonly words: VisitWords; readonly today: string },
  lookup: VisitLookup,
): VisitRowVm;
```

`ClientCardCatalog` (§5.2.1) structurally satisfies `VisitLookup`, so the dashboard builds one object. The staff outcome uses `subjectVisitOf(subject, appointment)`: **export** the private `outcomeOf` / `SubjectVisit` logic from `libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/client-stats.ts` (`:181-265`) as `subjectVisitOf(subject: UserId, appointment: Appointment): SubjectVisit | null` with the `SubjectVisit` and `SubjectVisitOutcome` types (the private `VisitOutcome` renamed on export, so it never clashes with `VisitRowOutcome`). Section labels use `Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, {month: 'long', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'Europe/Sofia'})` (month grain, capitalised in headings only, old R59) or the year number (year grain). The tile caption is the weekday under month grain and the short month under year grain (and on the glance), so the tile always says what the section does not.

#### 5.3.3 `lib-visit-list`, `lib-visit-row`, `lib-visit-filter`

```ts
@Component({ selector: 'lib-visit-list' })
export class VisitList {
  readonly sections = input.required<readonly VisitSectionVm[]>();
  readonly perspective = input.required<VisitPerspective>();
  readonly listStyle = input<VisitListStyle>('insetGrouped');
  readonly state = input<'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'>('ready');
  readonly skeletonRows = input(5);
  readonly paging = input<{
    readonly kind: 'more' | 'loading' | 'failed' | 'end';
    readonly endLabel: string | null;
  } | null>(null);
  readonly openRow = output<string>(); // client perspective only
  readonly loadMore = output<void>();
  readonly retry = output<void>();
  // <ng-content select="[visitListEmpty]" />: the host words its own empty
}
@Component({ selector: 'lib-visit-row' })
export class VisitRow {
  readonly vm = input.required<VisitRowVm>();
  readonly perspective = input.required<VisitPerspective>();
  readonly variant = input<VisitRowVariant>('row');
  readonly open = output<void>(); // client rows
  readonly openPhoto = output<string>(); // card thumbs
}
@Component({ selector: 'lib-visit-filter' })
export class VisitFilterMenu {
  readonly value = model<VisitFilter>('all');
  readonly perspective = input.required<VisitPerspective>();
  readonly testIdPrefix = input.required<string>(); // 'appointments-filter' (client, ids kept) · 'history-filter' (staff)
}
```

- **Sections.** Each section: `ui-section-header uiHeaderProminence="standard"` (`[uiPinned]` in `plain`; `h3` with `id` = `visit-section-{key}` and `data-section-key`) + the rows. `insetGrouped` wraps the rows in `ul[uiListGroup][aria-labelledby]` (the client, unchanged look); `plain` in `ul role="list"` with no segment fill (the staff history; the glance's H2/H3). The client's scroll spy observes `[data-section-key]` inside the host.
- **Row element.** Client → `button[uiListRow uiSize="regular" uiAlignment="leading" [uiInteractive]="true"]` with `data-testid="appointment-row"`, `[attr.data-appointment-id]`, `[attr.data-status]` (the ids the client page's specs and `apps/web/e2e/appointments.spec.ts` rely on); staff → `li[uiListRow uiSize="regular" uiAlignment="leading"]` (a read row, `data-testid="history-row-{id}"`).
- **Row body.** Leading `ui-date-tile` (or the waitlist glyph); `lib-staff-event-head size="regular" [title] [lines] [struck] [quote]`; trailing per `VisitTrailing`; marks as 16 px glyphs under the trailing figure, each with a visually hidden label.
- **`variant="card"`** (staff glance only): no `li`, rendered inside the consumer's `ui-card`; `ui-date-tile uiControlSize="large"`; a thumbs row of ≤3 `button[uiInteractive] > ui-media-card uiRatio="1 / 1" [uiScrim]="false"` at `calc(var(--sys-space-unit) * 18)`.
- **Filter.** The house funnel (`client-appointments.html:70-124`): `uiButton` bordered, neutral, 44, icon-only `action.filter`, accent while filtering, now over `ui-choice-menu` (the owner's one-choice-menu ruling, `choice-menu.ts:69-86`) instead of a hand-assembled `ui-menu` + `ui-list-group` + check glyph. One trigger grammar in both perspectives. Option words: `visits.filter.*` (moved from `appointments.filter.*`), with one perspective difference: the no-show option reads «Пропуснати» for the client and «Неявявания» for staff (`visits.filter.no_show` / `.no_show_staff`).
- **Paging.** `button[uiButton] bordered neutral regular [uiLoading]` «Покажи още» / «Опитай пак»; end line footnote. Empty: `ui-empty-state` with the projected copy. Error: `ui-notice` + retry.

#### 5.3.4 The staff perspective: row anatomy (supersedes old §5A.2.3's table, keeps its reasoning)

| subject outcome  | tile (caption / day)     | title                                                                                                                                                             | fact line (≤2 ·; never the date: the tile and section own it)                                                                                  | trailing                                                                                                                                                    | marks                                                  |
| ---------------- | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ |
| this appointment | its tone, «септ.» / «28» | plain                                                                                                                                                             | «днес, 10:00 · Иван»                                                                                                                           | `badge` «Този час»                                                                                                                                          | —                                                      |
| open, upcoming   | upcoming                 | plain                                                                                                                                                             | «пт, 10:00 · Иван» (weekday + time: the tile shows month and day)                                                                              | none (nothing paid yet)                                                                                                                                     | —                                                      |
| pending          | upcoming                 | plain                                                                                                                                                             | same                                                                                                                                           | `status` `visit.pending` «Заявен» (`appointments.status.pending`)                                                                                           | —                                                      |
| worked           | past                     | plain                                                                                                                                                             | «Иван · Център» (the shop only with >1 location)                                                                                               | `amount` charged, primary                                                                                                                                   | photos ×n (`client.photos`), discount (`promo.coupon`) |
| no_show          | past                     | **struck**, full ink                                                                                                                                              | «Иван»                                                                                                                                         | `status` `visit.noShow` «Не дойде» (`staff.visit.resolution.noShow`), glyph in `--sys-color-warning` (the warning is the glyph; the word stays primary ink) | —                                                      |
| cancelled        | hollow                   | **struck**, full ink (never struck AND faded: one mark per fact, and the fade took the tile caption and the struck amount under 4.5:1; WP4 review fix 2026-09-28) | «Иван · Отказан от клиента» / «… · Отказан от салона» (`staff.visit.resolution.cancelledByClient` / `.cancelledByStaff`, via `clientCaused()`) | `amount` booked, secondary, **struck**                                                                                                                      | —                                                      |

- **Price.** Worked: `Appointment.total()` when the subject booked a party of one; otherwise the subject's own seats' `terms.price` sum (no pro-rata of root discounts: owner question Q2) and the fact line gains «с още 1». Money is always `formatMoney` («27,50 €»).
- **Quote.** The client's own words (`contact.note`) as a line under the facts, only when this subject is the booker (`ownerUserIdOf`, or a NEW domain `Appointment.bookerId()`); absent for sysadmin (R15). The event head gains `quote: string | null` (NEW input), rendering `visit.note` glyph + «„…“» in the text face.
- **Barber.** Always in the fact line for staff.
- No row carries `›`; the accessible name carries every fact, including the status word, the price and the whole quote.

#### 5.3.5 The client perspective: how «Моите часове» keeps its behaviour

- `ClientAppointments` keeps its store, chrome, calendar view, pills, sheet and cancel flow. Its list view becomes `<lib-visit-list perspective="client" grain="month" listStyle="insetGrouped" [sections]="sections()" [state]="…" (openRow)="showVisit($event)">` with its two empties projected; its funnel becomes `<lib-visit-filter perspective="client" testIdPrefix="appointments-filter" [(value)]="filter">` in the same place, so `appointments-filter` and `appointments-filter-{option}` survive.
- The row keeps: tap → the detail sheet (by id from the page's map), `data-testid="appointment-row"`, `data-appointment-id`, `data-status`, the section anchors the pills jump to (now `[data-section-key]`), the upcoming accent stamp (now `ui-date-tile uiTone="upcoming"`, same wash), services deduplicated as the title, «Иван · 14:30» as the fact line.
- It changes (upgrades, listed so the build can update screenshots): the month heading is sentence case with the year («Септември 2026 г.», was «СЕПТЕМВРИ»); the barber's face moves to the trailing edge as `ui-avatar small`; cancelled and no-show rows are struck through (the sheet still shows the coloured status badge); the fact line uses the event head's mono face (owner question Q3); the verbs row becomes `ui-quick-actions` (Q4).
- `apps/web/e2e/appointments-screenshots.spec.ts` baselines are regenerated in wave D after the owner sees them.

#### 5.3.6 The event head move

`libs/features/staff/dashboard/src/lib/shared/event-head/staff-event-head.{ts,css,spec.ts}` → `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/event-head/` (same file names). Exported from `@creativo/features/shared/visits` as `StaffEventHead`, `StaffEventLine`, `StaffEventRun`, `StaffEventHeadSize`, `hollowStatus`. Importers updated: `staff-visit-editor.ts:86`, `staff-dashboard.ts:93`, `staff-time-grid.ts:27`; the two CSS comments that name the old path (`staff-dashboard.css:295`, `staff-time-grid.block.css:543`). NEW input `quote: string | null` (renders nothing when null: every existing consumer is unchanged). `staff-time-grid.spec.ts` and `staff-visit-editor.spec.ts`, which query `.staff-event-head__*`, stay green because the class names are kept.

**Ports** (exact signatures in §7.3): `libs/application/engagement/src/lib/ports/loyalty-reader.port.ts`, `loyalty-catalog-reader.port.ts`, with the documents and mappers in `loyalty-document.ts` (`*FromDocument`, each returning `Result`, going through the domain factories); `libs/application/booking/src/lib/ports/client-history-reader.port.ts`, `client-photos-reader.port.ts`. The store injects the ports directly (no use-case wrapper: a pass-through use case would be dead code). New ports are **new tokens**, not new methods on `APPOINTMENT_REPOSITORY` / `APPOINTMENT_PHOTOS` / `GIFT_VOUCHER_READER`: those interfaces have test fakes in five libs, and growing them would break other packages' specs mid-build.

Gift vouchers held by the client are **not** on the card in this build (no by-holder read; owner question Q1).

### 6.2 Wiring, rules, indexes, flags

- **Adapters register in the LAZY modules, never the barrels.** `libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/index.ts:1-5` and `libs/infrastructure/storage/src/index.ts:1-4` are eager-only by contract ("nothing here may import firebase/firestore as a value"); every adapter is reached through `loadFirestoreAdapters()` / `loadStorageAdapters()`. So the three Firestore adapters are added as `export *` lines to `libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/firestore.adapters.ts` and the photos reader to `libs/infrastructure/storage/src/lib/storage.adapters.ts`; the two `index.ts` files are untouched. `pnpm run verify:bundle` (`tools/verify/bundle/check-initial-bundle.mjs`) proves nothing leaked into the initial chunk.
- **`apps/web/src/app/app.config.ts`** gains four `provideLazyPort` bindings in the `firebaseData` scope (`onLoadError: dataUnavailable`), resolved from the lazy module as today (`:137-150`): `LOYALTY_READER` → `m.firestore.FirestoreLoyaltyReader` (`getAccount`, `listBadgeProgress`, `listSeasonProgress`, `listLedgerTail`: all `'promise'`); `LOYALTY_CATALOG_READER` → `m.firestore.FirestoreLoyaltyCatalogReader` (`getCatalog`); `CLIENT_HISTORY_READER` → `m.firestore.FirestoreClientHistoryReader` (`pageForSubject`); `CLIENT_PHOTOS_READER` → `m.storage.FirebaseClientPhotosReader` (`listForClient`, `countByAppointment`). `app.config.spec.ts` keeps proving that injecting every bound port loads nothing.
- **`firestore.rules`**: the six catalogue matches, `loyaltyAccounts/{uid}` with its two nested matches, `clientJournal/{entryId}` (§4.5). Default deny stays. `libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/firestore.rules.integration.spec.ts` gains: a barber reads Георги's account and progress ✓; a client reads their own account ✗ (until P5); `content_manager` ✗; any write ✗; a journal read of a `staff`-visibility row ✗; a journal query without the `visibility` filter ✗; any signed-in user reads the catalogue ✓, anonymous ✗. `libs/domain/accounts/src/lib/user-role.rules-parity.spec.ts:33` parses `firestore.rules` and must stay green.
- **`firestore.indexes.json`**: two composites (NEW): `clientJournal (userId ASC, visibility ASC, effect.kind ASC, seq DESC)` and `appointmentPhotos (clientUserId ASC, takenAtIso DESC)`. The history index exists. Both are deployed with the rules before production (memory "Firebase audit remediation").
- **Domain exports**: `libs/domain/engagement/src/lib/loyalty/*` via `libs/domain/engagement/src/index.ts`; `libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/client-stats.ts` exports `subjectVisitOf`, `SubjectVisit`, `SubjectVisitOutcome`. `@creativo/application/booking` already re-exports scheduling wholesale (`libs/application/booking/src/index.ts:34`); `@creativo/application/engagement` re-exports the loyalty types and functions **by name**. `libs/infrastructure/firestore/src/lib/firestore-paths.ts`'s note that "the never-wired per-user `achievements` subcollection is dropped" (`:42-43`) gains a line pointing at `loyaltyAccounts/{uid}/badgeProgress`, plus the new collection constants.
- **Flags**: `config/feature-flags.json` → `development`: `"client.profile": true`, `"loyalty.points": true`, `"loyalty.streaks": true`, `"loyalty.achievements": true`, `"loyalty.tiers": true`; `staging` and `production` stay false. The chain: `apps/web/src/environments/environment.ts:2` → `app.config.ts:239` `provideFeatureFlags(environment.featureFlags)` → the `FEATURE_FLAGS` token (`libs/application/shared/src/lib/feature-flags.token.ts:16`, factory default all-off). Read by exactly two places: `StaffVisitEditor` (`client.profile`) and `ClientCardStore` (the four loyalty flags). Specs turn flags on with `provideFeatureFlags({ 'client.profile': true, 'loyalty.points': true, … })` in the TestBed providers (partial maps parse; `FeatureFlags` itself lives in `@creativo/domain/governance`, which a `type:feature` spec may not import).
- **Editor wiring** (`staff-visit-editor.ts`/`.html`): the five page kinds and the stack (§2: `push`, `replace`, the generic `pop`, `popToLadder`); `providers: [ClientCardStore]`, **resolved lazily**: `private readonly injector = inject(Injector)` and `protected readonly card = signal<ClientCardStore | null>(null)`, set with `this.injector.get(ClientCardStore)` on the first `client` push while `client.profile` is on, so the ≈6,400-line editor spec and `staff-dashboard.spec.ts` never construct the store; then `card.open(subject, context)` on every `client` push (subject from `currentClient()`: `userId` = the id unless it starts with `guest-`; `primary` maps to `vm().clientUserId`; `isOwner` = `userId !== null && userId === uiOwnerUserId()`; `grants` = the editor's own `uiGrants` mapped through its coupon grammar (`couponValue`: the rule, none when the name says it, WP7 r6) when `isOwner`, else `[]` — those on the draft bill as `grantsOnBill`; `catalog` from the NEW `uiClientCardCatalog` input); `pageTitle()` reads `card.displayName()` for `client` and `card.barTitle(kind, subjectId)` for scope and detail pages; the `@case` branches mount the six bodies and map their outputs to `push` / `replace` / `popToLadder` / draft edits; `currentPhoto` falls back to `card.photoById` mapped to `VisitEditorPhoto` (§3.7); the read-only `photo` hides «Изтрий снимката»; the sheet's group labels move to `ui-section-header standard` (§5.1.3); the exits take `uiRole="destructive"` (§5.1.5); the two client-call glyphs (`staff-visit-editor.html:62`, `:2608`) move from `location.call` to `contact.phone`. Three NEW inputs bound by `staff-dashboard.html`: `uiOwnerUserId = input<string | null>(null)` (the appointment owner, the uid whose grants `probeGrants(row.ownerUserId)` already read, `staff-dashboard.ts:3922`: the VM carries only `clientUserId`, "what a booking is placed FOR", `staff-visit-editor.ts:327-328`), `uiViewer = input<{ uid: string; roles: readonly string[] } | null>(null)` (the signed-in principal: the money read and the ‹role› gates) and `uiClientCardCatalog = input<ClientCardCatalog | null>(null)` (built by the dashboard from its existing `CatalogContentService` / `CatalogPresenter`, `staff-dashboard.ts:1223-1224`).

## 7. Build plan: parallel agents in one working tree

### 7.0 Ground rules (every package)

1. **The tree is dirty and stays dirty.** It holds days of uncommitted work from other sessions: `app.config.ts`, `firestore.rules`, `firestore.indexes.json`, `tools/seed-dev-catalog.mjs`, `tsconfig.base.json`, both i18n files, `client-appointments.*`, `section-header.css`, the tokens' `project.json` and theme files are all modified, and `tools/seed/`, `config/feature-flags.json`, `accent-label.spec.ts` are untracked. Never `git checkout`, `stash`, `reset` or `clean`; undo by inverse edit.
2. **Re-read a file immediately before editing it**, make **additive, anchored edits** (the `Edit` tool on a unique anchor), never rewrite or regenerate a whole file, and never reformat code you did not write. The only generated file is `material-glyphs.generated.ts`, regenerated by its own script.
3. A package edits **only the files it owns** (§7.1). A needed change in someone else's file is a message to its owner, never an edit.
4. A package finishes every state it touches (no TODOs, no stubs) and runs its own gate (§7.2) before it reports done.
5. Template errors are invisible to `nx typecheck` (plain `tsc`, memory "typecheck misses templates"). A lib not yet imported by `apps/web` proves its templates with **`pnpm exec ngc -p <lib>/tsconfig.lib.json --outDir dist/out-tsc/ngc-check/<project>`** (`strictTemplates` is on in every feature lib's `tsconfig.json`) and with specs that **mount every component** under `TestBed.configureTestingModule({ errorOnUnknownElements: true, errorOnUnknownProperties: true })`.
6. **TypeScript project references are hand-edited by the owner of the importing project.** The feature and domain libs list their dependencies in `tsconfig.lib.json` / `tsconfig.spec.json` `references` (Nx 23's sync generator keeps them). A package that makes a project import a new lib adds that one reference line itself, in a file it owns (§7.1, §7.4); nobody runs a workspace-wide `nx sync` (it rewrites files other packages own); every gate ends with `pnpm nx sync:check` (a drift it names in a file outside this build's lists is reported to the lead, never fixed).

| --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ### 7.2 Waves and gates

Each wave starts only when the previous wave's gate is green. Within a wave, packages run in parallel against the §7.3 contracts.

| Wave                             | Packages (parallel)                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Gate (all green before the next wave)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **A0 · contracts first** (short) | WP2 tokens + icon keys; WP3 documents, fixtures, `testing.ts`, `model.ts`; WP4 i18n keys, `tsconfig.base.json` paths, the visits scaffold + `visit-row.vm.ts`; WP5 the client-card scaffold + `client-card.contracts.ts` | `pnpm icons:glyphs && pnpm icons:check`; `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint -p tokens icons i18n engagement`; `pnpm nx run-many -t lint -p shared-visits staff-client-card` (the scaffolds hold no specs yet)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **A1 · foundations**             | WP1; WP2 pieces; WP3 domain, ports, mappers, adapters, rules, indexes, wiring; WP4 sections, filter, presenter, event-head move; WP6 projector + seed writes                                                             | `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint -p patterns layout controls tokens icons shared-catalog domain-engagement domain-scheduling domain-accounts engagement booking firestore storage shared-visits staff-dashboard i18n seed-tools`; `pnpm run test:emulator` (the rules and every `*.integration.spec.ts`: they are excluded from `test`, `libs/infrastructure/firestore/vite.config.mts:17-20`); `pnpm icons:check`; `pnpm nx run showcase:build`; `pnpm exec ngc -p libs/features/shared/visits/tsconfig.lib.json --outDir dist/out-tsc/ngc-check/shared-visits` |
| **B1 · components**              | WP4 `lib-visit-row`, `lib-visit-list`, `lib-visit-filter`; WP5 store, presenters, tiles, strip and the four sub-page bodies; WP6 the mapper-parsing spec                                                                 | `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint -p shared-visits staff-client-card seed-tools`; both `ngc` checks (shared-visits, staff-client-card); `pnpm icons:check`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **B2 · composition**             | WP4 the client appointments migration; WP5 `lib-client-card`, `lib-client-history`                                                                                                                                       | `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint -p shared-visits staff-client-card client-appointments i18n`; both `ngc` checks; `pnpm nx run web:build` (compiles the client migration's templates); `pnpm icons:check`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **C · wiring**                   | WP7                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint -p staff-dashboard web`; `pnpm nx run web:build`; `pnpm run verify:bundle`; `pnpm icons:check`; `pnpm run test:emulator`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **D · verify**                   | WP8 (§7.6)                                                                                                                                                                                                               | §7.6                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### 7.5 Required test updates

- **`staff-visit-editor.spec.ts`**: the client-page cases at `:2885-2910` stay as the **flag-off** stub's tests. NEW cases under `provideFeatureFlags({ 'client.profile': true })` with the in-memory readers of both `/testing` entries: `lib-client-card` mounts; `#staff-visit-page-title` is the `h2` inside `ui-sheet-headline`; push to depth 2/3/4 and the replace at the cap; the related-medal replace keeps `returnTo`; ‹ restores scroll (after render) and focuses the origin; ✓ unwinds to the ladder and focuses the client row; a `client → clientLoyalty → discount → scan` chain returns to `clientLoyalty` (the `replace` invariant); the read-only photo's dock; «Пак същото» applies legs; the destructive row runs `removeClient`; a companion's card shows no ticket. The existing `clientNew`/`scan` cases keep their assertions, `:4983` (`depth()` is 1 with the scanner up) included.
- **`staff-block-editor.spec.ts`**: the exits render with `data-role="destructive"`; the armed row keeps its weight.
- **`libs/features/staff/dashboard/src/lib/stylesheets.spec.ts`**: **no change** (the pin must stay green: no CSS leaked into the editor).
- **`staff-dashboard.spec.ts`**: the `uiOwnerUserId`, `uiViewer` and `uiClientCardCatalog` bindings; nothing else.
- **`libs/ui/tokens/src/lib/accent-label.spec.ts` + `libs/ui/tokens/project.json`**: the date-stamp path moves to `date-tile.css`.
- **`client-appointments.spec.ts`**: selectors into row internals (`.visit__date`, `.visit__with`, section heads) change; the ids `appointment-row`, `data-appointment-id`, `data-status`, `appointments-list`, `appointments-pill`, `appointments-filter`, `appointments-filter-{option}` are kept, so the sheet, cancel and filter cases stay. `apps/web/e2e/appointments.spec.ts` stays green unchanged.
- The moved `staff-event-head.spec.ts` (+ a `quote` case); `staff-time-grid.spec.ts` unchanged (class names kept).
- **DS**: `sheet-headline.spec.ts`, `sheet-header.spec.ts` and `modal-sheet.spec.ts` (CSS contracts), NEW `section-header.spec.ts`, `list-row.spec.ts`, `scroll-row.spec.ts`, the badge spec, `slider.spec.ts`, the choice-tiles spec, NEW specs for the eight new pieces; `icon-vocabulary.spec.ts` after regeneration.
- **i18n** `catalogue-parity.spec.ts` (both files).
- **Data**: `firestore.rules.integration.spec.ts` (§6.2); the four adapters as `*.integration.spec.ts` (the house precedent, run by `pnpm run test:emulator`); the mapper specs parse the A0 fixtures; the domain specs (`badge-series`, `season`, `loyalty-catalog`, `standing`: the diversity rule, the day grouping, `familyCounts` per scope, `tierSteps`); `user-role.rules-parity.spec.ts` stays green.
- **Seed**: `tools/seed/loyalty.spec.mjs` asserts invariants: balance = Σ point-row deltas; «Посещения» `value` = `clientStats.lifetime.visitsCompleted`; every family's levels contiguous with rising targets; every projected document parses with WP3's mappers and has the fixtures' shape; exactly one open season, one open event and one ended season relative to the calendar's today; the loyalty catalogue is written under `--catalog-only`.
- **e2e**: NEW `apps/web/e2e/client-card-glance.spec.ts` (signs in a barber with `signInAs`, seeds a client with `seedUserProfile` + `seedWorkedAppointment` + `seedClientStats` + `seedLoyalty` + `seedAppointmentPhoto`, opens the visit, asserts the glance order H before F, the history page's year heads, a medal detail at depth 3, ✓ back to the ladder, and `expectNoAxeViolations` per theme). `appointments-screenshots.spec.ts` baselines are regenerated by WP8 once the owner has seen the new rows.

### 7.6 Wave D verification

1. `pnpm run seed:dev` against the emulators; `pnpm nx run-many -t test lint --all`; `pnpm run test:emulator`; `pnpm nx run web:build`; `pnpm run verify:bundle`; `pnpm icons:check`; `pnpm run e2e:web`.
2. Playwright at 330, 375 and 390 wide plus the ≥761 dialog, light and dark, signed in as `dev-staff-ivan` (memory "Staff preview sign-in": a minted custom token + `window.__e2eSignIn`), through every page of §2 for Георги and Мартин: glance; loyalty with each anchor; achievements; a category, a season, the archive, a day group; a detail at depth 4 and the replace at the cap; history with paging and each filter; a photo.
3. The §5.1.2 collapse sweep on the four named sheets; `expectNoAxeViolations` per theme on each page.
4. The glance budget recount (§3.0) from the 390 pt screenshots; the §9 pre-flight answered against them.
5. The client app's «Моите часове» and its visit sheet side by side with the old baselines; new baselines only after the owner's OK.

## 8. Copy keys and icon keys

### 8.1 i18n keys (`libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/assets/i18n/{bg,en}.json`, nested)

Family titles, level requirements, season and category names, tier and reward names are **data** (`LocalizedText`, §4.4), never keys. Dates follow old §5A.1.5 (R59: «12.08», the year only outside the current year, `month: 'long'` in accessible names); ranges use the house `Intl` range formatter (`dayRange` in `@creativo/features/shared/visits`, re-exported by `client-card.format.ts`; the season window and the waitlist's days alike, never a hand-built «8.10–9.10», wave D r6); points use `Intl.NumberFormat` («12 450 т.»); money `formatMoney` («27,50 €»). Plurals use the catalogue's `.one` / `.other` pair (`plural-form.ts`). **Reuse comes first**: a word the app already has is read from its existing key.

**Reused, not re-minted**

| use                       | existing key                                                     | copy                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| quick action «call»       | `staff.day.visitCall`                                            | Обади се                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| quick action «same again» | `appointments.visit.bookAgain`                                   | Пак същото (DV6)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| the ticket's action word  | `staff.visit.useNow`                                             | Използвай                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| the photos heading        | `staff.visit.photos`                                             | Снимки                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| a photo's name            | `staff.visit.photoOpen`                                          | Снимка от {{when}}                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| the destructive row       | `staff.visit.removeClient`                                       | **value changes**: «Махни» → «Махни от посещението» / "Remove" → "Remove from visit": one act, one word, on the row, the ladder's remove button and its swipe (their aria reads «Махни от посещението: Иво»; the editor spec carries its own map, `staff-visit-editor.spec.ts:181`). The walk-in is the outcome, never a label: «Без клиент (на място)» is dropped and `staff.visit.walkIn` «Случаен клиент» stays the seat's name (owner question Q10) |
| no-show status            | `staff.visit.resolution.noShow`                                  | Не дойде                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| pending status            | `appointments.status.pending`                                    | Заявен (stays in place: the dashboard reads `appointments.status.*` too, `staff-dashboard.ts:2175, 2858, 3844, 6168`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| the upcoming section      | `appointments.sections.upcoming`                                 | Предстоящи                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| filtered empty            | `appointments.empty.filtered`                                    | Няма посещения за този филтър.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

**Moved with the filter** (WP4 adds the copies in A0 and deletes the originals in B2 with the migration; only `client-appointments` reads them): `appointments.filter.{label, all, upcoming, completed, cancelled, no_show, clear}` → `visits.filter.*` (Филтър · Всички · Предстоящи · Минали · Отказани · Пропуснати · Покажи всички), plus NEW `visits.filter.no_show_staff` «Неявявания» / "No-shows" (the one word that differs per perspective).

| `clientCard.history.stats.noShows.one` / `.other`                        | неявяване / неявявания                                                       | no-show / no-shows                                                                                 |

| `clientCard.history.statsName`                                           | Общо: {{visits}}{{interval}}{{risk}}                                         | In total: {{visits}}{{interval}}{{risk}}                                                           |
| `clientCard.history.noRisk`                                              | Без неявявания и откази от клиента                                           | No no-shows or client cancellations                                                                |
| `clientCard.history.spend`                                               | Похарчени общо {{money}}                                                     | Spent in total {{money}}                                                                           |
| `clientCard.history.yearVisits.one` / `.other`                           | {{count}} посещение / {{count}} посещения                                    | {{count}} visit / {{count}} visits                                                                 |

| `visits.more` / `.moreFailed` / `.retry`                                 | Покажи още / Още посещения не се заредиха. / Опитай пак                      | Show more / More visits didn't load. / Try again (the one retry word, the card's notices included) |

**Dropped from draft 1** (duplicates of existing copy or retired): `staff.visit.client.walkIn`, `.remove`, `clientCard.actions.call`, `.same`, `clientCard.reward.use`, `clientCard.reward.fromStampCard`, `clientCard.photos.title`, `.photoName`, `clientCard.capsule.new`, `clientCard.pause.*`, `clientCard.history.filters.*`, `clientCard.history.empty.*`, `visits.upcoming`, `visits.status.*`, `clientCard.sliceFailed`, `clientCard.region.*`, `clientCard.retry`.

**P1 keys (the notes module; added when `CLIENT_NOTES` lands, not in this build):** `clientCard.notes.title` «Бележки», `clientCard.notes.stamp` «{{name}} · {{date}}», `clientCard.notes.tagAdd` «＋ Етикет», `clientCard.notes.pinned` «Закачена бележка», and old §5A.1.5's `clientCard.note.*` / `clientCard.tags.*` set.

### 8.2 Icon keys (`libs/ui/icons/src/lib/manifest/icon-vocabulary.ts`)

Every NEW glyph below was fetched at the pinned revision `27e9ef1` (Material Symbols Rounded, FILL 1, 24) on 2026-09-28 and has exactly one `<path>` on the `0 -960 960 960` viewBox, which `tools/icons/build-material-glyphs.mjs` requires (re-verified for this revision: `forum`, `stars`, `workspace_premium`, `event_repeat`, `ac_unit`, `approval`, `receipt_long`, `military_tech`, `award_star`, `celebration`, `inventory_2`, `flag`, `person_heart`, `star_shine`, `trophy`, `group_add`, `touch_app`, `cake`, `waving_hand`, `hotel_class`, `wb_twilight`, `weekend`, `alarm_on`, `person_check`, `explore`, `person_remove`, `cloud_off`, `loyalty`, `storefront`, `group`, `notifications_active`, `redeem`, `lock`, `photo_library`; added with the one-face-per-family review: `cycle`, `u_turn_left`, `date_range`, `luggage`; added for the shelf headings: `design_services`, `timeline`, and `signpost`, which replaced `stairs` in wave D r4 because FILL 1 draws `stairs` as a solid tile; added for the season and event headings, wave D r4: `calendar_clock`, `festival`). A key is registered only when this build renders it.

Glyph sizes: medal glyphs and monograms are **disc-derived** (`0.5 × disc`, the avatar fallback's precedent, `avatar.css:96-101`); every other glyph sits on the 16/20/24 ladder. Deferred with their surfaces (not registered now): `client.alert` (`keep`, P1), `client.note` / `client.tag` (P1), `client.profile` (`contact_page`) and `nav.leave` (`arrow_outward`) with the full-profile route. ## 9. Taste pre-flight, answered for this design (`taste-digest.md` §c)

| #   | Check                                                                             | Answer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | The head is the DS head                                                           | Yes: `ui-sheet-headline` + `h2 uiSheetLargeTitle id="staff-visit-page-title"`; the avatar (and on the detail page the medal) rides the media slot; zero page-local bar, head or fade CSS; the collapse is a cross-fade on one timeline (§5.1.2). No page here has a search, so `titleCollapsed` stays unbound |
| 2   | Avatar and name sized as a pair                                                   | `large` 52 beside `title2` 24/700 on a 52 pt text floor: a one-line name + line (48.6 pt) balances the disc; a longer name grows below while the disc stays on its first line. CSS only, no measurement; no new avatar rung (D1)                                                                              |
| 3   | ≤4 head elements, no redundant state                                              | avatar, name, one state line, one capsule row. The pause is the ring + the line (no third signal); a new client is the line (no «Нов» capsule); the visit count lives only in H0                                                                                                                              |
| 4   | Each domain its own composition, ≤2 consecutive list groups, no single-row groups | capsules, tiles, ticket, featured card + plain rows, bento, medal strip, rail; the glance has one list group (the exits, D2's exception); history is a plain list under sticky year heads (DV2); the medal ladder is a step gauge, not a list; the archive is a lone `neutral` row, not a group               |
| 5   | Achievements scale to hundreds                                                    | the glance holds one strip (3 medals + one cue); the page: diversity-ruled rails, family shelves with their own art, a season card, an events rail, records, a one-row archive; scope pages section by service category; counts in the unit drawn                                                             |
| 6   | Domains separated; one primary figure each; the reward usable now first           | points, stamps, rhythm and achievements each have their own place and one figure; the tier is a capsule, never a metric; the ticket sits above the visits and the loyalty module                                                                                                                              |
| 7   | Visits reuse the client list                                                      | `lib-visit-list` / `lib-visit-row` / `lib-visit-filter` shared with «Моите часове»; one filter grammar, one vocabulary                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 8   | One accent                                                                        | fills `--sys-color-accent` (meters, medals, the ticket value, the upcoming tile wash), text ink `--sys-color-accent-label`: one orange (§5.1.15); tiers and rarity by form, never hue alone; no gradients but the chrome fades; no hex in feature CSS                                                         |
| 9   | Surfaces                                                                          | muted `ui-card` / list segments only; max two framing levels; translucent `--sys-color-fill` on tiles, not-started medals, past tiles; no local `box-shadow` or `border` in features; no overlay CSS in features (the photo date is `ui-media-card`'s caption slot)                                           |
| 10  | Type only through tokens; a real ladder                                           | name 24/700 > figures 20/600 > headings 18/600 > row titles 14/600 > facts 12 > captions 11; `uiFont` roles only; nothing under `caption`; every pushed page's title one size, the sheet's `largeTitle`, and `title2` only on a head with a face (DV7, resolved)                                              |
| 11  | Registry icons on the ladder                                                      | §8.2 verified at the pin; `nav.disclosure` pushes, `action.filter` filters; one key per intent (`contact.phone`, `client.photos`)                                                                                                                                                                             |
| 12  | Interaction grammar                                                               | tiles, cards, medals, thumbs and the ticket carry `uiInteractive` / `data-interactive` with the ungated `:active` mirror; buttons 36/44/52; ‹ steps back, ✓ leaves the client; tiles push at different anchors                                                                                                |
| 13  | Every state                                                                       | the §3.x state tables: shaped skeletons, one retry grammar (compact notice under the module's heading), offline notice, absent-not-disabled, paused first, a production-flags wireframe (§3.1.1)                                                                                                              |
| 14  | Motivated motion only                                                             | the collapse, push/pop, press, value changes after settle (M7–M16); no mount animation, no staggered reveals, no celebration for staff                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 15  | Copy audit                                                                        | Bulgarian sentence case, plain names, no exclamation marks, ≤2 «·» per line, no fact printed twice on one screen, dates only where they are the answer, «спиране»/«спряно» never «блокиран», «в ритъм» never «серия»                                                                                          |
| 16  | Themes and widths                                                                 | tokens in both themes; 330/375/390 and the ≥761 dialog per page; WP8 screenshots both themes                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 17  | No tells                                                                          | no eyebrows, no decorative dots (the paused ring is semantic), no button look on a label (the photo date is a badge), meters are real progress, figures come from data                                                                                                                                        |
| 18  | Reuse audit                                                                       | every missing capability is a DS extension or a new DS piece with a spec and a showcase page (§5.1); the house exits recipe, the tile face and the mark grammar were promoted, not copied; copy reuses existing keys first (§8.1)                                                                             |
| 19  | Complete output                                                                   | each package finishes its states; gates per wave (§7.2); verification §7.6                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 20  | Data is real                                                                      | real ports for everything but loyalty; loyalty is the seeded engine output derived from the seeded history; absent slices hide their module                                                                                                                                                                   |
| 21  | **Glance budget** (NEW)                                                           | ≤ 1,400 pt of content from the head's top and ≤ 40 visible text runs (figures inside graphics excluded) at 390 pt, development seed: measured on the wireframe 1,385 pt and 34 runs (§3.1.1); WP8 recounts from screenshots                                                                                   |

## 10. Owner questions (each defaulted so the build is not blocked)

| #   | Question                                                                                                              | Default used by this spec                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Q0  | The deviations DV1–DV8 (§0)                                                                                           | Built as written in §0; each has a one-line fallback                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Q1  | Should gift-voucher balances the client holds appear with the ready rewards?                                          | No, not in this build (no by-holder read)                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Q2  | A party visit's price for one person: their own seats' prices, a pro-rata share of the discount, or the party total?  | Their own seats' prices, the discount on the booker's row only, plus «с още N»                                                                                                                                                |
| Q3  | The client's «Моите часове» rows adopt the staff event head's mono fact line?                                         | Yes; screenshots go to the owner before the baselines are replaced                                                                                                                                                            |
| Q4  | The client sheet's verbs row becomes the neutral quick-action tiles (the accent discs go)?                            | Yes (one component, one look)                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Q5  | Events: dates only, or also «още N дни»?                                                                              | Dates only («до 5.10»); no countdown (UCPD)                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Q6  | What does a numeral on a medal mean?                                                                                  | The highest earned level, always; the target only in words in the caption                                                                                                                                                     |
| Q7  | Do a season's levels count toward the earned figure?                                                                  | Yes («34 нива» counts every earned level, seasons included)                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Q8  | The ✓ on client sub-pages goes straight back to the visit (‹ steps back)?                                             | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Q9  | A companion's held rewards: where?                                                                                    | Nowhere in this build: a grant applies only to its owner's appointment (server rule), so the card shows the ticket to the owner only; a read-only list for companions is a follow-up that needs a shared rule-label formatter |
| Q10 | «Махни» becomes «Махни от посещението» on the ladder too (one act, one word), and «Без клиент (на място)» is dropped? | Yes                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

**Follow-ups, deliberately out of this build:** promote the editor's own photo tile (`staff-visit-editor.html:2279-2286`, `staff-visit-editor.visit.css:168-200`) to `ui-media-card`; delete `appointment-groups.ts` and `calendar-month.ts` in `client-appointments` (imported only by their specs); companions' held rewards (Q9); the notes module and tags page (P1); the full-profile route and its row; the pause notice with by-whom, why and actions (P2).

## Appendix: Critique resolution

Three reviews of draft 1 (2026-09-28): **HR** house rules and DS reuse (`design-critique-house-rules.md`), **TH** taste, HIG and Mobbin (`design-critique-taste-hig.md`), **FE** feasibility and build plan (`design-critique-feasibility.md`). Every blocker and major was checked against the working tree before it was acted on; every evidence line cited below was confirmed. Verdicts: **accepted**, **accepted, resolved differently** (the defect is fixed by another route, reason given), **partly accepted** (reason for the part not taken), **rejected** (reason given). No blocker or major is rejected.

### A.1 House rules and DS reuse (HR)

| ----- | ------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| HR-1  | blocker | History gets a second filter and a second vocabulary                                    | **Accepted** (verified: `client-appointments.html:70-124`, `appointments.filter.*`, `choice-menu.ts:69-86`) | The union, predicate and menu move into `shared/visits` (`visit-filter.ts`, `lib-visit-filter` on `ui-choice-menu`); one trigger grammar (the house funnel) in both perspectives, the client's test ids kept; `appointments.filter.*` move to `visits.filter.*` with one perspective word (`no_show_staff`); status and section words reuse `appointments.status.*` / `appointments.sections.upcoming` / `staff.visit.resolution.*` in place (the dashboard reads them too). §3.6 HD, §5.3.1, §5.3.3, §8.1                              |
| HR-2  | major   | Destructive row copies the exits group with the wrong ink                               | **Accepted** (verified: `modifiers.css:103-105` vs `staff-visit-editor.sections.css:322-336`)               | `uiRole="destructive"` on `uiListRow` (`-label` ink, armed weight) in WP1; the visit and block editors' exits migrate in WP7 and the feature rule is deleted; the card mounts it with no `uiForegroundStyle`. §5.1.5, §3.1.2 K, §7.1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

One act, one word: the destructive row reuses `staff.visit.removeClient` (value «Махни от посещението»); «Без клиент (на място)» dropped; «Случаен клиент» stays the seat's name (Q10). The "all NEW" claim is gone; a "dropped from draft 1" list is added |

### A.4 What the revision changed in the build plan, in one place

- Waves are now **A0 (contracts) → A1 (foundations) → B1 (components) → B2 (composition) → C (wiring) → D (verify)**, each with its own gate commands (§7.2).
- Ownership is disjoint and complete: every file a package touches is listed under exactly one package, including the formerly unowned `firestore.adapters.ts`, `storage.adapters.ts`, `firestore-paths.ts`, `model.ts`, both `/testing` entries, `seed-firestore.ts`, the block editor, `choice-tiles.css`, the slider and `cr-capsule-list` (§7.1, §7.4).
- Contracts carry exact signatures for every port, pure builder and component, and the A0 fixtures make the wire format a file rather than a promise (§4.4, §7.3).
- Every hotspot has one owner and one wave; the event-head lines in the dashboard are edited in A1 only, before WP7 owns those files in C (§7.4).
